Utilities today already have rigorous frameworks for capital planning, and there are opportunities to extend this capability to solve multi-objective optimization problems in the face of uncertainty. This work presents a four-part framework that 1) incorporates extreme weather as a source of uncertainty, 2) leverages a digital twin of the grid, 3) uses Monte Carlo simulation to capture variability and 4) applies a multi-objective optimization method for finding the optimal investment portfolio. We use this framework to investigate whether grid-aware optimization methods outperform model-free approaches. We find that, in fact, given the computational complexity of model-based metaheuristic optimization methods, the simpler net present value ranking method was able to find more optimal portfolios with only limited knowledge of the grid.

</details>


### [114] [Interpretable Deep Reinforcement Learning for Element-level Bridge Life-cycle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02528)
*Seyyed Amirhossein Moayyedi,David Y. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可解释强化学习的桥梁生命周期优化策略方法，针对2022年新规范下元素级状态数据的高维状态空间问题。该方法通过软决策树模型、温度退火和正则化剪枝技术，生成可解释的斜决策树策略，应用于钢梁桥的生命周期优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年新实施的《国家桥梁清单规范》(SNBI)采用元素级条件状态(CS)比例数据替代传统单一评级，虽提升了数据粒度，但将状态空间从单一分类整数扩展为四维概率数组，给生命周期优化策略的制定带来巨大挑战。

Method: 提出一种可解释强化学习方法：(a)使用可微分软树模型作为行动者函数逼近器；(b)训练过程中引入温度退火；(c)结合正则化与剪枝规则限制策略复杂度，最终生成斜决策树形式的确定性策略。

Result: 该方法生成的策略以斜决策树形式呈现，具有合理的节点数量和深度，可被人理解、审计，并易于集成到现有桥梁管理系统中。在监督学习和强化学习场景中验证了各技术的效果与权衡。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功应用于钢梁桥生命周期优化问题，证明了可解释强化学习方法在桥梁资产管理系统中处理高维状态空间的可行性，为实现数据驱动的可解释决策提供了新途径。

Abstract: The new Specifications for the National Bridge Inventory (SNBI), in effect from 2022, emphasize the use of element-level condition states (CS) for risk-based bridge management. Instead of a general component rating, element-level condition data use an array of relative CS quantities (i.e., CS proportions) to represent the condition of a bridge. Although this greatly increases the granularity of bridge condition data, it introduces challenges to set up optimal life-cycle policies due to the expanded state space from one single categorical integer to four-dimensional probability arrays. This study proposes a new interpretable reinforcement learning (RL) approach to seek optimal life-cycle policies based on element-level state representations. Compared to existing RL methods, the proposed algorithm yields life-cycle policies in the form of oblique decision trees with reasonable amounts of nodes and depth, making them directly understandable and auditable by humans and easily implementable into current bridge management systems. To achieve near-optimal policies, the proposed approach introduces three major improvements to existing RL methods: (a) the use of differentiable soft tree models as actor function approximators, (b) a temperature annealing process during training, and (c) regularization paired with pruning rules to limit policy complexity. Collectively, these improvements can yield interpretable life-cycle policies in the form of deterministic oblique decision trees. The benefits and trade-offs from these techniques are demonstrated in both supervised and reinforcement learning settings. The resulting framework is illustrated in a life-cycle optimization problem for steel girder bridges.

</details>


### [115] [Competency Questions as Executable Plans: a Controlled RAG Architecture for Cultural Heritage Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02545)
*Naga Sowjanya Barla,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于知识图谱的神经符号架构，通过将传统设计时验证工具"能力问题"重新用于运行时叙事规划，解决大语言模型在文化遗产叙事生成中的幻觉问题，实现可验证、可审计的故事生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非物质文化遗产的保护面临集体记忆随时间消退的严峻挑战，而大语言模型虽能生成吸引人的叙事内容，但其"幻觉"问题导致事实准确性无法保证，难以满足文化遗产应用中"真实性"的核心要求。

Method: 提出"规划-检索-生成"的神经符号架构：(1) 将能力问题(CQs)从设计时验证工具重新定义为运行时可执行的叙事计划；(2) 在Live Aid KG多模态数据集上系统比较三种RAG策略：纯符号KG-RAG、文本增强混合RAG、结构感知Graph-RAG。

Result: 三种RAG策略存在可量化的权衡：符号检索具有最高事实精度，混合方法提供更丰富的上下文，Graph-RAG在叙事连贯性上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计个性化、可控的文化遗产叙事系统提供了可操作的见解，证明神经符号方法能有效平衡生成质量与事实准确性。

Abstract: The preservation of intangible cultural heritage is a critical challenge as collective memory fades over time. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for generating engaging narratives, their propensity for factual inaccuracies or "hallucinations" makes them unreliable for heritage applications where veracity is a central requirement. To address this, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic architecture grounded in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) that establishes a transparent "plan-retrieve-generate" workflow for story generation. A key novelty of our approach is the repurposing of competency questions (CQs) - traditionally design-time validation artifacts - into run-time executable narrative plans. This approach bridges the gap between high-level user personas and atomic knowledge retrieval, ensuring that generation is evidence-closed and fully auditable. We validate this architecture using a new resource: the Live Aid KG, a multimodal dataset aligning 1985 concert data with the Music Meta Ontology and linking to external multimedia assets. We present a systematic comparative evaluation of three distinct Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) strategies over this graph: a purely symbolic KG-RAG, a text-enriched Hybrid-RAG, and a structure-aware Graph-RAG. Our experiments reveal a quantifiable trade-off between the factual precision of symbolic retrieval, the contextual richness of hybrid methods, and the narrative coherence of graph-based traversal. Our findings offer actionable insights for designing personalised and controllable storytelling systems.

</details>


### [116] [Mitigating LLM biases toward spurious social contexts using direct preference optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02585)
*Hyunji Nam,Dorottya Demszky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在教师教学质量评估中对虚假语境信息的敏感性，发现模型预测可偏移达1.48分（7分制），且标准缓解方法效果有限。提出的Debiasing-DPO方法通过配对中性推理与有偏推理进行自监督训练，可将偏差降低84%并提升准确率52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险决策场景（如教师教学质量评估）中，对虚假语境信息的敏感性可能导致有害偏见，进而影响教师的职业发展。此外，模型规模化未必带来鲁棒性提升，标准缓解方法（如提示工程和DPO）效果有限。

Method: 使用NCTE数据集（美国课堂转录最大公开数据集）评估7个前沿及开源模型在7类虚假语境下的表现。提出Debiasing-DPO方法：配对仅基于查询的中性推理与基于查询+虚假语境的有偏推理进行自监督训练，并结合基于真实标签的监督微调以防准确率下降。

Result: 虚假语境信息可使模型预测偏移达1.48分（7分制），大模型有时反而更敏感。Debiasing-DPO应用于Llama 3B/8B和Qwen 3B/7B后，平均偏差降低84%，准确率提升52%。

Conclusion: 模型规模化并非提升鲁棒性的自然途径；Debiasing-DPO等自监督方法可有效提升基于提示的预测任务的准确性与鲁棒性，为高风险决策场景中的LLM部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for high-stakes decision-making, yet their sensitivity to spurious contextual information can introduce harmful biases. This is a critical concern when models are deployed for tasks like evaluating teachers' instructional quality, where biased assessment can affect teachers' professional development and career trajectories. We investigate model robustness to spurious social contexts using the largest publicly available dataset of U.S. classroom transcripts (NCTE) paired with expert rubric scores. Evaluating seven frontier and open-weight models across seven categories of spurious contexts -- including teacher experience, education level, demographic identity, and sycophancy-inducing framings -- we find that irrelevant contextual information can shift model predictions by up to 1.48 points on a 7-point scale, with larger models sometimes exhibiting greater sensitivity despite higher predictive accuracy. Mitigations using prompts and standard direct preference optimization (DPO) prove largely insufficient. We propose **Debiasing-DPO**,, a self-supervised training method that pairs neutral reasoning generated from the query alone, with the model's biased reasoning generated with both the query and additional spurious context. We further combine this objective with supervised fine-tuning on ground-truth labels to prevent losses in predictive accuracy. Applied to Llama 3B \& 8B and Qwen 3B \& 7B Instruct models, Debiasing-DPO reduces bias by 84\% and improves predictive accuracy by 52\% on average. Our findings from the educational case study highlight that robustness to spurious context is not a natural byproduct of model scaling and that our proposed method can yield substantial gains in both accuracy and robustness for prompt-based prediction tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Do Audio-Visual Large Language Models Really See and Hear?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02605)
*Ramaneswaran Selvakumar,Kaousheik Jayakumar,S Sakshi,Sreyan Ghosh,Ruohan Gao,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对音频-视觉大语言模型(AVLLMs)进行机制可解释性研究，揭示了AVLLMs在训练过程中存在严重的多模态偏差问题：尽管模型中间层编码了丰富的音频语义，但在深层融合层中，视觉表征过度主导，导致音频线索被抑制。这种偏差源于训练过程中缺乏对音频的有效对齐监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AVLLMs成为多模态感知统一接口的发展趋势，理解其内部工作机制变得至关重要。该研究旨在回答一个核心问题：AVLLMs如何在不同层级处理、融合音频和视觉信息，以及为什么会出现模态偏差现象。

Method: 采用机制可解释性(mechanistic interpretability)方法，通过探测分析(probing analysis)追踪音频和视觉特征在模型各层的演化过程，并对比AVLLM与其视觉-语言基模型的音频行为差异。

Result: 发现AVLLMs在中间层确实编码了丰富的音频语义，但当音频与视觉信息冲突时，这些能力在最终文本生成中大多失效。深层融合层不成比例地倾向于视觉表征，从而抑制音频线索。进一步追溯发现，AVLLM的音频行为与其视觉-语言基模型高度一致，表明模型缺乏对音频监督的有效对齐训练。

Conclusion: AVLLMs存在根本性的模态偏差问题，其根源在于训练阶段的音频对齐不足。这一发现为理解和改进多模态LLMs的音视频整合机制提供了新的理论依据和实践方向。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AVLLMs) are emerging as unified interfaces to multimodal perception. We present the first mechanistic interpretability study of AVLLMs, analyzing how audio and visual features evolve and fuse through different layers of an AVLLM to produce the final text outputs. We find that although AVLLMs encode rich audio semantics at intermediate layers, these capabilities largely fail to surface in the final text generation when audio conflicts with vision. Probing analyses show that useful latent audio information is present, but deeper fusion layers disproportionately privilege visual representations that tend to suppress audio cues. We further trace this imbalance to training: the AVLLM's audio behavior strongly matches its vision-language base model, indicating limited additional alignment to audio supervision. Our findings reveal a fundamental modality bias in AVLLMs and provide new mechanistic insights into how multimodal LLMs integrate audio and vision.

</details>


### [118] [OntoKG: Ontology-Oriented Knowledge Graph Construction with Intrinsic-Relational Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02618)
*Yitao Li,Zhanlin Liu,Anuranjan Pandey,Muni Srikanth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种本体导向的知识图谱组织方法，通过内在-关系路由机制将属性分类并路由到对应模式模块。该方法基于Wikidata 2026年1月 dump，创建了包含3400万实体和6120万边的属性图，模式可跨存储后端移植并在5个下游应用中独立复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱构建方法将模式决策嵌入pipeline代码或临时抽取关系，导致模式与构建过程紧耦合，难以复用于下游本体级任务。需要一种从设计之初就面向本体分析、实体消歧、领域定制和LLM引导抽取的模式架构。

Method: 核心机制是内在-关系路由（intrinsic-relational routing），将每个属性分类为内在属性或关系属性并路由到对应模式模块。流程包括：基于规则的清洗阶段从Wikidata dump识别3460万核心实体集，然后进行迭代路由将每个属性分配到94个模块（8大类）。结合工具增强的LLM支持和人工审查完成分类。

Result: 模式达到93.3%的类别覆盖率和98.0%的模块分配准确率。导出产生3400万节点、6120万边、38种关系类型的属性图。5个独立应用验证了模式的可复用性：本体结构分析、基准注解审计、实体消歧、领域定制和LLM引导抽取。

Conclusion: 内在-关系路由机制成功实现了声明式、可移植且独立可复用的模式设计。该方法证明了本体导向模式可脱离构建pipeline，独立服务于多种下游任务，为知识图谱的模块化和可扩展性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Organizing a large-scale knowledge graph into a typed property graph requires structural decisions -- which entities become nodes, which properties become edges, and what schema governs these choices. Existing approaches embed these decisions in pipeline code or extract relations ad hoc, producing schemas that are tightly coupled to their construction process and difficult to reuse for downstream ontology-level tasks. We present an ontology-oriented approach in which the schema is designed from the outset for ontology analysis, entity disambiguation, domain customization, and LLM-guided extraction -- not merely as a byproduct of graph building. The core mechanism is intrinsic-relational routing, which classifies every property as either intrinsic or relational and routes it to the corresponding schema module. This routing produces a declarative schema that is portable across storage backends and independently reusable.
  We instantiate the approach on the January 2026 Wikidata dump. A rule-based cleaning stage identifies a 34.6M-entity core set from the full dump, followed by iterative intrinsic-relational routing that assigns each property to one of 94 modules organized into 8 categories. With tool-augmented LLM support and human review, the schema reaches 93.3% category coverage and 98.0% module assignment among classified entities. Exporting this schema yields a property graph with 34.0M nodes and 61.2M edges across 38 relationship types. We validate the ontology-oriented claim through five applications that consume the schema independently of the construction pipeline: ontology structure analysis, benchmark annotation auditing, entity disambiguation, domain customization, and LLM-guided extraction.

</details>


### [119] [Let's Have a Conversation: Designing and Evaluating LLM Agents for Interactive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02666)
*Joshua Drossman,Alexandre Jacquillat,Sébastien Martin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展、可复现的评估方法，通过对话交互来评估优化智能体。研究者构建了基于LLM的决策智能体，模拟不同利益相关者，在学校排课案例中生成数千次对话。研究表明：一对一评估严重受限，通过对话交互的优化智能体能收敛到更高质量的解决方案；结合领域特定提示和结构化工具的定制化优化智能体比通用聊天机器人表现显著更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题的建模与求解同样重要，但传统一次性评估方法存在局限。随着大语言模型（LLM）驱动的人机交互式优化智能体兴起，如何可靠评估这类对话式交互系统成为关键挑战——对话交互的评估远比传统单次方法困难，缺乏标准化评估框架制约了该领域发展。

Method: 研究者构建了LLM驱动的决策智能体，通过角色扮演模拟多样化利益相关者，每个智能体受内部效用函数驱动但以真实决策者方式沟通。在学校排课案例研究中生成数千次对话，横向比较：①一次性评估 vs 对话式交互的效果差异；②通用聊天机器人 vs 领域定制化优化智能体的性能表现。

Result: 研究发现：1）一次性评估具有严重局限性，同一优化智能体通过对话交互能收敛到更高质量解决方案；2）配备领域特定提示和结构化工具的定制优化智能体，相比通用聊天机器人，在更少交互轮次内实现显著更优的解质量。

Conclusion: 研究证实了AI-优化交叉领域新兴解决方案的实践价值，有助于扩大优化技术的应用范围。同时揭示了运筹学专业知识在设计有效可靠的优化智能体中的关键作用，为交互式部署的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Optimization is as much about modeling the right problem as solving it. Identifying the right objectives, constraints, and trade-offs demands extensive interaction between researchers and stakeholders. Large language models can empower decision-makers with optimization capabilities through interactive optimization agents that can propose, interpret and refine solutions. However, it is fundamentally harder to evaluate a conversation-based interaction than traditional one-shot approaches. This paper proposes a scalable and replicable methodology for evaluating optimization agents through conversations. We build LLM-powered decision agents that role-play diverse stakeholders, each governed by an internal utility function but communicating like a real decision-maker. We generate thousands of conversations in a school scheduling case study. Results show that one-shot evaluation is severely limiting: the same optimization agent converges to much higher-quality solutions through conversations. Then, this paper uses this methodology to demonstrate that tailored optimization agents, endowed with domain-specific prompts and structured tools, can lead to significant improvements in solution quality in fewer interactions, as compared to general-purpose chatbots. These findings provide evidence of the benefits of emerging solutions at the AI-optimization interface to expand the reach of optimization technologies in practice. They also uncover the impact of operations research expertise to facilitate interactive deployments through the design of effective and reliable optimization agents.

</details>


### [120] [GrandCode: Achieving Grandmaster Level in Competitive Programming via Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02721)
*DeepReinforce Team,Xiaoya Li,Xiaofei Sun,Guoyin Wang,Songqiao Su,Chris Shum,Jiwei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrandCode是首个在实时竞赛中持续击败所有人类选手的多智能体强化学习编程系统，标志着AI在竞赛编程领域超越最强人类程序员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程能力快速提升，但在竞赛编程这一最具挑战性的编码任务中仍落后于人类最强选手。即便如Google Gemini Ultra 3 Deep Think这样的顶尖AI系统，其表现也未达到人类顶尖水平，且未在实时竞赛条件下接受评估。竞赛编程作为评估AI编程能力的终极标杆，亟需突破性解决方案。

Method: GrandCode采用多智能体强化学习架构，包含假设提议、智能体求解、测试生成、摘要等多个模块，通过后训练和在线测试时RL联合优化。核心创新在于设计了专门针对多阶段智能体rollout、延迟奖励及严重离策略漂移问题的Agentic GRPO算法。

Result: GrandCode在最近三场Codeforces实时竞赛（2026年3月21日Round 1087、3月28日Round 1088、3月29日Round 1089）中均获第一名，击败包括传奇大师在内的所有人人类选手，成为首个在实时竞赛条件下持续超越全体人类参赛者的AI系统。

Conclusion: AI系统已在最竞争性的编程任务上全面超越最强人类程序员，竞赛编程这一AI编程能力的终极堡垒已被攻克。这标志着AI编程能力达到新的里程碑。

Abstract: Competitive programming remains one of the last few human strongholds in coding against AI. The best AI system to date still underperforms the best humans competitive programming: the most recent best result, Google's Gemini~3 Deep Think, attained 8th place even not being evaluated under live competition conditions. In this work, we introduce GrandCode, a multi-agent RL system designed for competitive programming. The capability of GrandCode is attributed to two key factors: (1) It orchestrates a variety of agentic modules (hypothesis proposal, solver, test generator, summarization, etc) and jointly improves them through post-training and online test-time RL; (2) We introduce Agentic GRPO specifically designed for multi-stage agent rollouts with delayed rewards and the severe off-policy drift that is prevalent in agentic RL. GrandCode is the first AI system that consistently beats all human participants in live contests of competitive programming: in the most recent three Codeforces live competitions, i.e., Round~1087 (Mar 21, 2026), Round~1088 (Mar 28, 2026), and Round~1089 (Mar 29, 2026), GrandCode placed first in all of them, beating all human participants, including legendary grandmasters. GrandCode shows that AI systems have reached a point where they surpass the strongest human programmers on the most competitive coding tasks.

</details>


### [121] [DeltaLogic: Minimal Premise Edits Reveal Belief-Revision Failures in Logical Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02733)
*Amit Dhanda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeltaLogic是一个基准测试转换协议，将自然语言推理示例转换为短小的信念修正任务，评估模型在最小证据变化下的信念更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理基准测试仅评估模型在固定前提下的推理能力，但无法衡量动态环境中更重要的能力——最小证据变化下的信念修正能力。

Method: 将FOLIO和ProofWriter中的自然语言推理示例转换为"修订片段"：先基于前提P得出初始结论，再施加最小编辑δ(P)，最后判断结论是否需要修正。采用约束标签评分评估小型因果语言模型。

Result: 更强的初始推理能力并不必然带来更强的修正行为。Qwen3-1.7B初始准确率0.667但修正准确率仅0.467；Qwen3-4B呈现惯性失败模式(初始0.650→修正0.450，惯性0.600)；Phi-4-mini-instruct表现更强(0.950→0.850)但仍有非平凡的弃权和控制不稳定性。

Conclusion: 固定前提下的逻辑能力不等于局部证据编辑后的自律信念修正能力。DeltaLogic填补了逻辑推理与信念更新基准之间的空白，揭示了当前模型在信念修正上的显著不足。

Abstract: Reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate whether a model derives the correct answer from a fixed premise set, but they under-measure a closely related capability that matters in dynamic environments: belief revision under minimal evidence change. We introduce DeltaLogic, a benchmark transformation protocol that converts natural-language reasoning examples into short revision episodes. Each episode first asks for an initial conclusion under premises P, then applies a minimal edit δ(P), and finally asks whether the previous conclusion should remain stable or be revised. We instantiate DeltaLogic from FOLIO and ProofWriter and evaluate small causal language models with constrained label scoring. On a completed 30-episode Qwen evaluation subset, stronger initial reasoning still does not imply stronger revision behavior: Qwen3-1.7B reaches 0.667 initial accuracy but only 0.467 revision accuracy, with inertia rising to 0.600 on episodes where the gold label should change, while Qwen3-0.6B collapses into near universal abstention. There, Qwen3-4B preserves the same inertial failure pattern (0.650 initial, 0.450 revised, 0.600 inertia), whereas Phi-4-mini-instruct is substantially stronger (0.950 initial, 0.850 revised) but still exhibits non-trivial abstention and control instability. These results suggest that logical competence under fixed premises does not imply disciplined belief revision after local evidence edits. DeltaLogic therefore targets a distinct and practically important reasoning capability that complements existing logical inference and belief-updating benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [Aligning Progress and Feasibility: A Neuro-Symbolic Dual Memory Framework for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02734)
*Bin Wen,Ruoxuan Zhang,Yang Chen,Hongxia Xie,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong potential in long-horizon decision-making tasks, such as embodied manipulation and web interaction. However, agents frequently struggle with endless trial-and-error loops or deviate from the main objective in complex environments. We attribute these failures to two fundamental errors: global Progress Drift and local Feasibility Violation. Existing methods typically attempt to address both issues simultaneously using a single paradigm. However, these two challenges are fundamentally distinct: the former relies on fuzzy semantic planning, while the latter demands strict logical constraints and state validation. The inherent limitations of such a single-paradigm approach pose a fundamental challenge for existing models in handling long-horizon tasks. Motivated by this insight, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic Dual Memory Framework that explicitly decouples semantic progress guidance from logical feasibility verification. Specifically, during the inference phase, the framework invokes both memory mechanisms synchronously: on one hand, a neural-network-based Progress Memory extracts semantic blueprints from successful trajectories to guide global task advancement; on the other hand, a symbolic-logic-based Feasibility Memory utilizes executable Python verification functions synthesized from failed transitions to perform strict logical validation. Experiments demonstrate that this method significantly outperforms existing competitive baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and TextCraft, while drastically reducing the invalid action rate and average trajectory length.

</details>


### [123] [Improving Role Consistency in Multi-Agent Collaboration via Quantitative Role Clarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02770)
*Guoling Zhou,Wenpei Han,Fengqin Yang,Li Wang,Yingcong Zhou,Zhiguo Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In large language model (LLM)-driven multi-agent systems, disobey role specification (failure to adhere to the defined responsibilities and constraints of an assigned role, potentially leading to an agent behaving like another) is a major failure mode \cite{DBLP:journals/corr/abs-2503-13657}. To address this issue, in the present paper, we propose a quantitative role clarity to improve role consistency. Firstly, we construct a role assignment matrix $S(φ)=[s_{ij}(φ)]$, where $s_{ij}(φ)$ is the semantic similarity between the $i$-th agent's behavior trajectory and the $j$-th agent's role description. Then we define role clarity matrix $M(φ)$ as $\text{softmax}(S(φ))-I$, where $\text{softmax}(S(φ))$ is a row-wise softmax of $S(φ)$ and $I$ is the identity matrix. The Frobenius norm of $M(φ)$ quantifies the alignment between agents' role descriptions and their behaviors trajectory. Moreover, we employ the role clarity matrix as a regularizer during lightweight fine-tuning to improve role consistency, thereby improving end-to-end task performance. Experiments on the ChatDev multi-agent system show that our method substantially improves role consistency and task performance: with Qwen and Llama, the role overstepping rate decreases from $46.4\%$ to $8.4\%$ and from $43.4\%$ to $0.2\%$, respectively, and the role clarity score increases from $0.5328$ to $0.9097$ and from $0.5007$ to $0.8530$, respectively, the task success rate increases from $0.6769$ to $0.6909$ and from $0.6174$ to $0.6763$, respectively.

</details>


### [124] [CharTool: Tool-Integrated Visual Reasoning for Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02794)
*Situo Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Zichen Zhu,Da Ma,Lei Pan,Danyang Zhang,Zihan Zhao,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DuoChart数据管道和CharTool工具增强系统，通过智能体强化学习提升多模态大语言模型在图表推理任务上的表现。CharTool-7B在多个图表基准上超越基线模型超8%，并展现良好的领域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表在科学和金融文献中广泛使用，但多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在图表推理方面面临挑战，主要原因是缺乏高质量训练数据，以及需要细粒度视觉定位和精确数值计算能力。

Method: 1) 提出DuoChart——可扩展的双源数据管道，结合合成图表与真实图表构建多样化、高质量的图表训练数据；2) 开发CharTool——为MLLMs配备外部工具，包括用于局部视觉感知的图像裁剪和用于精确数值推理的代码执行；3) 通过DuoChart上的智能体强化学习(agentic reinforcement learning)训练，使CharTool学会工具整合的图表内容推理。

Result: 在六个图表基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在不同模型规模上均一致优于强基线MLLMs。CharTool-7B在CharXiv(Reasoning)上提升+8.0%，在ChartQAPro上提升+9.78%，达到与更大或专有模型相当的竞争性性能。

Conclusion: CharTool展示了向领域外视觉数学推理基准的正向泛化能力，验证了工具整合推理方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Charts are ubiquitous in scientific and financial literature for presenting structured data. However, chart reasoning remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to the lack of high-quality training data, as well as the need for fine-grained visual grounding and precise numerical computation. To address these challenges, we first propose DuoChart, a scalable dual-source data pipeline that combines synthesized charts with real-world charts to construct diverse, high-quality chart training data. We then introduce CharTool, which equips MLLMs with external tools, including image cropping for localized visual perception and code-based computation for accurate numerical reasoning. Through agentic reinforcement learning on DuoChart, CharTool learns tool-integrated reasoning grounded in chart content. Extensive experiments on six chart benchmarks show that our method consistently improves over strong MLLM baselines across model scales. Notably, CharTool-7B outperforms the base model by **+8.0%** on CharXiv (Reasoning) and **+9.78%** on ChartQAPro, while achieving competitive performance with substantially larger or proprietary models. Moreover, CharTool demonstrates positive generalization to out-of-domain visual math reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [125] [EMS: Multi-Agent Voting via Efficient Majority-then-Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02863)
*Yiqing Liu,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EMS框架，通过将多智能体投票重新定义为可靠性感知调度问题，结合智能体置信度建模、增量投票和动态更新三项技术，实现提前终止推理，在保持决策质量的同时平均减少32%的智能体调用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多数投票方法需等待所有智能体完成推理后再聚合，导致计算资源浪费——一旦达成多数共识，后续智能体的响应变得冗余。

Method: EMS框架包含三个核心组件：1) Agent Confidence Modeling (ACM) 利用历史表现和语义相似度评估智能体可靠性；2) Adaptive Incremental Voting (AIV) 顺序选择智能体并实现早期停止；3) Individual Confidence Updating (ICU) 动态更新贡献智能体的可靠性评分。

Result: 在六个基准测试上的实验表明，EMS一致性地将平均调用的智能体数量减少32%。

Conclusion: EMS通过任务感知可靠性调度和自适应停止机制，有效提升了多智能体推理效率，同时保持了决策的多数一致性。

Abstract: Majority voting is the standard for aggregating multi-agent responses into a final decision. However, traditional methods typically require all agents to complete their reasoning before aggregation begins, leading to significant computational overhead, as many responses become redundant once a majority consensus is achieved. In this work, we formulate the multi-agent voting as a reliability-aware agent scheduling problem, and propose an Efficient Majority-then-Stopping (EMS) to improve reasoning efficiency. EMS prioritizes agents based on task-aware reliability and terminates the reasoning pipeline the moment a majority is achieved from the following three critical components. Specifically, we introduce Agent Confidence Modeling (ACM) to estimate agent reliability using historical performance and semantic similarity, Adaptive Incremental Voting (AIV) to sequentially select agents with early stopping, and Individual Confidence Updating (ICU) to dynamically update the reliability of each contributing agent. Extensive evaluations across six benchmarks demonstrate that EMS consistently reduces the average number of invoked agents by 32%.

</details>


### [126] [Analysis of Optimality of Large Language Models on Planning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02910)
*Bernd Bohnet,Michael C. Mozer,Kevin Swersky,Wil Cunningham,Aaron Parisi,Kathleen Kenealy,Noah Fiedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了大语言模型在经典AI规划问题（Blocksworld）中是否能够进行最优规划，而非仅依赖简单启发式策略。研究发现推理增强型LLM在复杂多目标场景下显著优于传统满意规划器（如LAMA），且能近乎完美地追踪理论最优性界限，即使在去除领域特定语义线索后仍能保持性能，并提出"算法模拟"和"几何记忆"两种机制来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM规划基准测试主要关注成功率而忽视规划效率，导致无法区分模型是进行真正最优推理还是使用简单但低效的策略。这一研究空白促使作者系统性地评估前沿模型在规划效率和最优性方面的表现。

Method: 1) 使用Blocksworld领域（涉及标定方块塔的移动规划）作为核心测试场景；2) 引入广义Path-Star (P*) 图任务以隔离纯拓扑推理能力；3) 系统性操控问题深度（塔高）、宽度（塔数量）和组合性（目标方块数）三个维度；4) 对比推理增强型LLM与传统satisficing规划器（LAMA）的性能。

Result: 推理增强型LLM在复杂多目标配置中显著优于LAMA规划器。随着搜索空间指数级扩展，经典搜索算法性能急剧下降，而LLM能近乎完美地追踪理论最优性界限，即便去除领域语义提示后仍保持此能力。

Conclusion: LLM展现出超越传统规划器的规划能力，其背后存在两种可能的机制：1) 通过推理token实现的主动算法模拟；2) 能将P*拓扑表征为可导航全局几何结构的"几何记忆"，从而有效绕过指数级组合复杂度。

Abstract: Classic AI planning problems have been revisited in the Large Language Model (LLM) era, with a focus of recent benchmarks on success rates rather than plan efficiency. We examine the degree to which frontier models reason optimally versus relying on simple, heuristic, and possibly inefficient strategies. We focus on the Blocksworld domain involving towers of labeled blocks which have to be moved from an initial to a goal configuration via a set of primitive actions. We also study a formally equivalent task, the generalized Path-Star ($P^*$) graph, in order to isolate true topological reasoning from semantic priors. We systematically manipulate problem depth (the height of block towers), width (the number of towers), and compositionality (the number of goal blocks). Reasoning-enhanced LLMs significantly outperform traditional satisficing planners (e.g., LAMA) in complex, multi-goal configurations. Although classical search algorithms hit a wall as the search space expands, LLMs track theoretical optimality limits with near-perfect precision, even when domain-specific semantic hints are stripped away. To explain these surprising findings, we consider (and find evidence to support) two hypotheses: an active Algorithmic Simulation executed via reasoning tokens and a Geometric Memory that allows models to represent the $P^*$ topology as a navigable global geometry, effectively bypassing exponential combinatorial complexity.

</details>


### [127] [AgentHazard: A Benchmark for Evaluating Harmful Behavior in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02947)
*Yunhao Feng,Yifan Ding,Yingshui Tan,Xingjun Ma,Yige Li,Yutao Wu,Yifeng Gao,Kun Zhai,Yanming Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentHazard基准，用于评估计算机使用智能体的有害行为，包含2,653个测试实例，涵盖多种风险类别和攻击策略。实验表明当前系统存在高度脆弱性，特别是在Qwen3-Coder驱动的Claude Code上攻击成功率高达73.63%，揭示了模型对齐本身不足以保证自主智能体的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体将语言模型从文本生成扩展到对工具、文件和执行环境的持久操作。与对话系统不同，它们在交互间保持状态并通过中间输出转化为具体行动。这创造了一个独特的安全挑战：有害行为可能通过一系列看似合理的步骤组合出现，包括仅看局部似乎可接受的中间行动，但整体上会导致未授权行为。现有安全评估方法难以捕捉这种"累积性危害"。

Method: 构建AgentHazard基准（2,653个实例），涵盖多种风险类别和攻击策略。每个实例将有害目标与一系列操作步骤配对，这些步骤在局部合法但整体诱导不安全行为。评估智能体识别和中断由累积上下文、重复工具使用、中间行动和跨步骤依赖性引发的危害的能力。使用Claude Code、OpenClaw和IFlow对Qwen3、Kimi、GLM和DeepSeek系列的开源模型进行测试。

Result: 当前系统表现出高度脆弱性。当使用Qwen3-Coder驱动Claude Code时，攻击成功率达73.63%。这表明仅依靠模型对齐无法可靠保证自主智能体的安全。

Conclusion: 模型对齐（alignment）本身不足以保证自主智能体的安全性。智能体安全需要超越传统对齐方法的专门机制，以应对累积性、跨步骤的有害行为。该研究强调了开发专门针对智能体安全评估和防护的紧迫性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents extend language models from text generation to persistent action over tools, files, and execution environments. Unlike chat systems, they maintain state across interactions and translate intermediate outputs into concrete actions. This creates a distinct safety challenge in that harmful behavior may emerge through sequences of individually plausible steps, including intermediate actions that appear locally acceptable but collectively lead to unauthorized actions. We present \textbf{AgentHazard}, a benchmark for evaluating harmful behavior in computer-use agents. AgentHazard contains \textbf{2,653} instances spanning diverse risk categories and attack strategies. Each instance pairs a harmful objective with a sequence of operational steps that are locally legitimate but jointly induce unsafe behavior. The benchmark evaluates whether agents can recognize and interrupt harm arising from accumulated context, repeated tool use, intermediate actions, and dependencies across steps. We evaluate AgentHazard on Claude Code, OpenClaw, and IFlow using mostly open or openly deployable models from the Qwen3, Kimi, GLM, and DeepSeek families. Our experimental results indicate that current systems remain highly vulnerable. In particular, when powered by Qwen3-Coder, Claude Code exhibits an attack success rate of \textbf{73.63\%}, suggesting that model alignment alone does not reliably guarantee the safety of autonomous agents.

</details>


### [128] [FoE: Forest of Errors Makes the First Solution the Best in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02967)
*Kehan Jiang,Haonan Dong,Zhaolu Kang,Zhengzhou Zhu,Guojie Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现了大语言推理模型中的"First is The Best"现象：首个解决方案优于后续替代方案。通过将错误建模为"错误森林"(FoE)结构，理论分析表明推理路径中的错误随测试时间累积增长。基于此提出了RED框架，包含Refining First和Discarding Subs两个组件，在5个基准、6个模型上实现最高19.0%的性能提升，同时减少37.7%~70.4%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的测试时扩展定律假设生成多个替代解决方案可以提升性能，但本文发现后续解决方案并非次优，反而可能有害。这一"第一个是最好的"(The First is The Best)现象挑战了现有范式，需要重新理解LRM的推理错误机制。

Method: 1) 通过综合实证分析将错误表征为森林结构的"错误森林"(FoE)；2) 提供严格理论分析证明FoE导致首个解最优；3) 提出RED自引导高效推理框架，包含：Refining First（抑制首个解中的FoE增长）和Discarding Subs（通过双重一致性剪枝后续FoE）。

Result: 在5个基准和6个骨干模型上，RED相比8个竞争基线表现更优，实现最高19.0%的性能提升，同时减少37.7%~70.4%的token消耗。消融实验验证了FoE指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 错误森林(FoE)是导致"第一个是最好的"现象的根本原因。基于此洞察的RED框架在有效性和效率上均显著超越现有方法，为大语言推理模型的测试时扩展提供了新的理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: Recent Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) like DeepSeek-R1 have demonstrated remarkable success in complex reasoning tasks, exhibiting human-like patterns in exploring multiple alternative solutions. Upon closer inspection, however, we uncover a surprising phenomenon: The First is The Best, where alternative solutions are not merely suboptimal but potentially detrimental. This observation challenges widely accepted test-time scaling laws, leading us to hypothesize that errors within the reasoning path scale concurrently with test time. Through comprehensive empirical analysis, we characterize errors as a forest-structured Forest of Errors (FoE) and conclude that FoE makes the First the Best, which is underpinned by rigorous theoretical analysis. Leveraging these insights, we propose RED, a self-guided efficient reasoning framework comprising two components: I) Refining First, which suppresses FoE growth in the first solution; and II) Discarding Subs, which prunes subsequent FoE via dual-consistency. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks and six backbone models demonstrate that RED outperforms eight competitive baselines, achieving performance gains of up to 19.0% while reducing token consumption by 37.7% ~ 70.4%. Moreover, comparative experiments on FoE metrics shed light on how RED achieves effectiveness.

</details>


### [129] [InfoSeeker: A Scalable Hierarchical Parallel Agent Framework for Web Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02971)
*Ka Yiu Lee,Yuxuan Huang,Zhiyuan He,Huichi Zhou,Weilin Luo,Kun Shao,Meng Fang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoSeeker框架，通过分层架构（Host-Managers-Workers）解决大模型智能体在海量信息聚合任务中的上下文饱和、错误传播和高延迟问题，在两个基准上实现3-5倍加速和显著的效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic搜索系统虽在多步推理上有进展，但在海量异构证据聚合方面存在严重局限：上下文饱和、错误级联传播和高延迟，难以应对数据密集型场景。

Method: 提出基于"近可分解性"原则的分层框架InfoSeeker，包含战略层Host、多管理器Managers和并行Workers三层。Manager层通过聚合和反思机制实现严格的上下文隔离，防止饱和和错误传播；Worker层并行处理加速任务执行。

Result: 效率：3-5倍加速；效果：WideSearch-en上8.4%成功率，BrowseComp-zh上52.9%准确率。

Conclusion: 分层架构结合上下文隔离与并行处理可有效解决数据密集型搜索中的性能瓶颈，为大规模信息合成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent agentic search systems have made substantial progress by emphasising deep, multi-step reasoning. However, this focus often overlooks the challenges of wide-scale information synthesis, where agents must aggregate large volumes of heterogeneous evidence across many sources. As a result, most existing large language model agent systems face severe limitations in data-intensive settings, including context saturation, cascading error propagation, and high end-to-end latency. To address these challenges, we present \framework, a hierarchical framework based on principle of near-decomposability, containing a strategic \textit{Host}, multiple \textit{Managers} and parallel \textit{Workers}. By leveraging aggregation and reflection mechanisms at the Manager layer, our framework enforces strict context isolation to prevent saturation and error propagation. Simultaneously, the parallelism in worker layer accelerates the speed of overall task execution, mitigating the significant latency. Our evaluation on two complementary benchmarks demonstrates both efficiency ($ 3-5 \times$ speed-up) and effectiveness, achieving a $8.4\%$ success rate on WideSearch-en and $52.9\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-zh. The code is released at https://github.com/agent-on-the-fly/InfoSeeker

</details>


### [130] [Agentic-MME: What Agentic Capability Really Brings to Multimodal Intelligence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03016)
*Qianshan Wei,Yishan Yang,Siyi Wang,Jinglin Chen,Binyu Wang,Jiaming Wang,Shuang Chen,Zechen Li,Yang Shi,Yuqi Tang,Weining Wang,Yi Yu,Chaoyou Fu,Qi Li,Yi-Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentic-MME是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)作为智能体的过程验证基准。该基准包含418个真实世界任务，涵盖6个领域和3个难度等级，通过超过2000个逐步检查点进行细粒度过程验证。结果显示最佳模型Gemini3-pro仅达到56.3%整体准确率，在Level-3任务上更是低至23.0%，表明真实世界的多模态智能体问题解决仍具极大挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs评估存在三大缺陷：(1)缺乏灵活的工具集成能力测试；(2)视觉工具和搜索工具分开测试；(3)主要通过最终答案评估，忽视过程验证。因此无法验证工具是否被正确调用、应用是否正确、以及使用效率高低。

Method: Agentic-MME基准包含418个真实世界任务，跨越6个领域和3个难度等级。核心特点包括：(1)统一的评估框架，支持沙箱代码执行和API调用；(2)人类参考轨迹标注，包含双轴检查点(S轴和V轴)；(3)细粒度中间状态审计，而非仅评估最终答案；(4)"过度思考"指标量化效率。

Result: 最佳模型Gemini3-pro整体准确率仅56.3%，在Level-3困难任务上骤降至23.0%。每个任务平均需要超过10人时的手工标注，共包含超过2000个逐步检查点。

Conclusion: 真实世界的多模态智能体问题解决具有极高难度，即使是目前最强的模型在复杂任务上表现仍不理想。该研究为评估MLLMs的工具调用能力提供了更全面、更细粒度的验证框架。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are evolving from passive observers into active agents, solving problems through Visual Expansion (invoking visual tools) and Knowledge Expansion (open-web search). However, existing evaluations fall short: they lack flexible tool integration, test visual and search tools separately, and evaluate primarily by final answers. Consequently, they cannot verify if tools were actually invoked, applied correctly, or used efficiently. To address this, we introduce Agentic-MME, a process-verified benchmark for Multimodal Agentic Capabilities. It contains 418 real-world tasks across 6 domains and 3 difficulty levels to evaluate capability synergy, featuring over 2,000 stepwise checkpoints that average 10+ person-hours of manual annotation per task. Each task includes a unified evaluation framework supporting sandboxed code and APIs, alongside a human reference trajectory annotated with stepwise checkpoints along dual-axis: S-axis and V-axis. To enable true process-level verification, we audit fine-grained intermediate states rather than just final answers, and quantify efficiency via an overthinking metric relative to human trajectories. Experimental results show the best model, Gemini3-pro, achieves 56.3% overall accuracy, which falls significantly to 23.0% on Level-3 tasks, underscoring the difficulty of real-world multimodal agentic problem solving.

</details>


### [131] [Automatic Textbook Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03071)
*Fabian Gloeckle,Ahmad Rammal,Charles Arnal,Remi Munos,Vivien Cabannes,Gabriel Synnaeve,Amaury Hayat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个AI系统将500多页的研究生代数组合学教材正式化到Lean证明助手中的案例研究。通过3万个Claude 4.5 Opus智能体并行协作，在一周内完成了13万行代码和5900个Lean声明的正式化，树立了教科书正式化的规模和能力新里程碑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在定理证明器正式化方面的工作仅停留在本科拓扑学水平和现有库内容的重构，尚未实现对完整研究生教材的独立正式化。本研究旨在展示AI系统能够规模化完成完整研究生教材的正式化工作。

Method: 采用3万个Claude 4.5 Opus智能体通过版本控制并行协作于共享代码库，在一周内对500多页的研究生代数组合学教材进行Lean正式化。实验同时创下多智能体软件工程的有用结果纪录。

Result: 成功完成13万行代码和5900个Lean声明的正式化，在教科书正式化规模和能力上树立新里程碑。推理成本与人类专家团队薪资相当或更低，且仍有较大效率提升空间。

Conclusion: 该研究标志着教科书正式化规模和多智能体软件工程能力的重大突破，证明了AI驱动正式化方法的成本竞争力，为未来进一步优化效率奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a case study where an automatic AI system formalizes a textbook with more than 500 pages of graduate-level algebraic combinatorics to Lean. The resulting formalization represents a new milestone in textbook formalization scale and proficiency, moving from early results in undergraduate topology and restructuring of existing library content to a full standalone formalization of a graduate textbook. The formalization comprises 130K lines of code and 5900 Lean declarations and was conducted within one week by a total of 30K Claude 4.5 Opus agents collaborating in parallel on a shared code base via version control, simultaneously setting a record in multi-agent software engineering with usable results. The inference cost matches or undercuts what we estimate as the salaries required for a team of human experts, and we expect there is still the potential for large efficiencies to be made without the need for better models. We make our code, the resulting Lean code base and a side-by-side blueprint website available open-source.

</details>


### [132] [Chart-RL: Policy Optimization Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced Visual Reasoning in Chart Question Answering with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03157)
*Yunfei Bai,Amit Dhanda,Shekhar Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chart-RL是一种基于强化学习的框架，通过策略优化和自适应奖励函数提升视觉语言模型在图表问答任务中的表现，仅需单GPU配置即可完成训练。经RL微调的Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct模型准确率达0.634，超越参数量两倍的Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct基础模型（0.580），同时推理延迟从31秒降至9秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在图表问答（CQA）任务中存在三大核心瓶颈：数值提取不精确、隐式视觉关系解读困难、以及空间关系注意力机制不足。这些局限性阻碍了VLMs在复杂数据可视化任务中的真正智能推理能力。

Method: 提出Chart-RL框架，核心创新包含三个方面：1）集成策略优化（Policy Optimization）技术与自适应奖励函数；2）引入LoRA参数高效微调，实现单GPU配置下的训练；3）在ChartQAPro数据集上对开源、专有及闭源SOTA模型进行广泛基准测试。

Result: RL微调的Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct达到0.634准确率，超越Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct基础模型（0.580），同时推理延迟从31秒降至9秒。

Conclusion: Chart-RL框架验证了强化学习与参数高效微调结合在图表理解任务中的有效性，以更少的参数量实现更优性能和更低延迟，为小型化、高效能VLMs提供了新路径。

Abstract: The recent advancements in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated progress toward true intelligence requiring robust reasoning capabilities. Beyond pattern recognition, linguistic reasoning must integrate with visual comprehension, particularly for Chart Question Answering (CQA) tasks involving complex data visualizations. Current VLMs face significant limitations in CQA, including imprecise numerical extraction, difficulty interpreting implicit visual relationships, and inadequate attention mechanisms for capturing spatial relationships in charts. In this work, we address these challenges by presenting Chart-RL, a novel reinforcement learning framework that enhances VLMs chart understanding through feedback-driven policy optimization of visual perception and logical inference. Our key innovation includes a comprehensive framework integrating Reinforcement Learning (RL) from Policy Optimization techniques along with adaptive reward functions, that demonstrates superior performance compared to baseline foundation models and competitive results against larger state-of-the-art architectures. We also integrated Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning through Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) in the RL framework that only requires single GPU configurations while preserving performance integrity. We conducted extensive benchmarking across open-source, proprietary, and state-of-the-art closed-source models utilizing the ChartQAPro dataset. The RL fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct model achieved an answer accuracy of 0.634, surpassing the 0.580 accuracy of the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct foundation model despite utilizing half the parameter count, while simultaneously reducing inference latency from 31 seconds to 9 seconds.

</details>


### [133] [Coupled Control, Structured Memory, and Verifiable Action in Agentic AI (SCRAT -- Stochastic Control with Retrieval and Auditable Trajectories): A Comparative Perspective from Squirrel Locomotion and Scatter-Hoarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03201)
*Maximiliano Armesto,Christophe Kolb*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用松鼠生态学作为理解Agentic AI系统的比较框架，因为松鼠的树栖运动、分散储食和情境感知型埋藏行为将控制、记忆和可验证行动三种需求整合于同一生物体中。作者构建了包含潜在动态、情景记忆、观察者信念状态、选项级动作和延迟验证信号的分层部分可观测控制模型，并推导出三个关于反馈鲁棒性、记忆组织和验证机制的假设，以及关于角色分化系统的下游猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI研究通常孤立处理控制、记忆或对齐问题，而自然界中松鼠的树栖运动、分散储食和情境感知型埋藏行为恰好将这三项核心需求耦合于同一生物体，提供了一个完整的比较案例来研究部分可观测性、延迟和策略性观察条件下的智能行为。

Method: 研究采用跨物种综合方法，整合狐狸、松鼠和东部灰松鼠的行为证据，并建立包含潜在动态、情景记忆、观察者信念状态、选项级动作和延迟验证信号的最小分层部分可观测控制模型。

Result: 推导出三个可检验假设：(H1)快速局部反馈加预测补偿可提高隐藏动态变化下的鲁棒性；(H2)面向未来控制的记忆组织可改善线索冲突和负载下的延迟检索；(H3)行动-记忆循环中的验证器和观察者模型可减少静默失败和信息泄露，但易受错误指定影响。提出角色分化提议者/执行者/检查者/对手系统可减少不对称信息和验证负担下的相关错误。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提供跨学科比较视角和基准议程，建立一套关于控制、记忆和可验证行动耦合关系的可证伪声明体系，为设计更鲁棒的Agentic AI系统提供生物启发的理论框架。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly judged not by fluent output alone but by whether it can act, remember, and verify under partial observability, delay, and strategic observation. Existing research often studies these demands separately: robotics emphasizes control, retrieval systems emphasize memory, and alignment or assurance work emphasizes checking and oversight. This article argues that squirrel ecology offers a sharp comparative case because arboreal locomotion, scatter-hoarding, and audience-sensitive caching couple all three demands in one organism. We synthesize evidence from fox, eastern gray, and, in one field comparison, red squirrels, and impose an explicit inference ladder: empirical observation, minimal computational inference, and AI design conjecture. We introduce a minimal hierarchical partially observed control model with latent dynamics, structured episodic memory, observer-belief state, option-level actions, and delayed verifier signals. This motivates three hypotheses: (H1) fast local feedback plus predictive compensation improves robustness under hidden dynamics shifts; (H2) memory organized for future control improves delayed retrieval under cue conflict and load; and (H3) verifiers and observer models inside the action-memory loop reduce silent failure and information leakage while remaining vulnerable to misspecification. A downstream conjecture is that role-differentiated proposer/executor/checker/adversary systems may reduce correlated error under asymmetric information and verification burden. The contribution is a comparative perspective and benchmark agenda: a disciplined program of falsifiable claims about the coupling of control, memory, and verifiable action.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [OmniTQA: A Cost-Aware System for Hybrid Query Processing over Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02444)
*Nima Shahbazi,Seiji Maekawa,Nikita Bhutani,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OmniTQA是一个成本感知的混合查询处理框架，用于同时处理结构化和半结构化数据。它将语义推理作为一等查询算子，与经典关系算子集成到可执行的DAG中，并结合数据感知规划、最小化语义工作负载。实验表明，该框架在准确性和成本效率上优于现有基线方法，特别适用于复杂查询、大规模表和多关系模式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL和表格问答系统假设查询相关信息都显式存在于结构化模式中。然而，企业数据库通常包含混合模式，结构化属性与自由形式文本字段共存，现有系统难以同时对两类信息进行推理。

Method: OmniTQA框架：(1) 将语义推理作为一等查询算子，无缝集成LLM语义操作与经典关系算子，形成可执行DAG；(2) 扩展经典查询优化，引入数据感知规划，结合原子查询分解与算子重排序以最小化语义工作负载；(3) 双引擎执行架构，动态路由任务至关系数据库与LLM模块，采用算子感知批处理实现高效扩展。

Result: 在多种结构化和半结构化表格问答基准测试中，OmniTQA在准确性和成本效率上持续优于现有符号方法、语义方法及混合基线。这些优势在复杂查询、大规模表和多关系模式场景下尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了纯符号化查询处理与纯语义推理之间的鸿沟，为企业混合数据库场景下的智能问答提供了可行方案。其成本感知设计使LLM推理的高延迟和成本问题得到有效缓解。

Abstract: While recent advances in large language models have significantly improved Text-to-SQL and table question answering systems, most existing approaches assume that all query-relevant information is explicitly represented in structured schemas. In practice, many enterprise databases contain hybrid schemas where structured attributes coexist with free-form textual fields, requiring systems to reason over both types of information. To address this challenge, we introduce OmniTQA, a cost-aware hybrid query processing framework that operates over both structured and semi-structured data. OmniTQA treats semantic reasoning as a first-class query operator, seamlessly integrating LLM-based semantic operations with classical relational operators into an executable directed acyclic graph. To manage the high latency and cost of LLM inference, it extends classical query optimization with data-aware planning, combining atomic query decomposition and operator reordering to minimize semantic workload. The framework also features a dual-engine execution architecture that dynamically routes tasks between a relational database and an LLM module, using operator-aware batching to scale efficiently. Extensive experiments across a diverse suite of structured and semi-structured table question answering benchmarks demonstrate that OmniTQA consistently outperforms existing symbolic, semantic, and hybrid baselines in both accuracy and cost efficiency. These gains are particularly pronounced for complex queries, large tables and multi-relation schemas.

</details>


### [135] [Efficient Path Query Processing in Relational Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02553)
*Diego Rivera Correa,Mirek Riedewald*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCAP，一种在关系型数据库中实现属性图路径查询早期过滤的抽象框架。通过将NFA状态转换和用户定义聚合函数的思想结合，使标准关系型数据库（如DuckDB）能够将属性约束深入下推到查询计划中，在多种查询和图数据上比当前最先进的图数据库和关系数据库快达400,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的图数据库和关系数据库都无法有效处理结合正则表达式标签和属性值约束的一般路径查询，主要问题在于无法在中间结果阶段进行早期过滤（early filtering），即当存在无法完成有效路径的可能性时及时淘汰中间结果。这导致大量无用计算浪费，影响查询效率。

Method: 提出ReCAP（Regular expression with Constraints on Attributes of Paths）抽象框架，其核心思想是"给予关系优化器一点帮助"——通过在查询中显式指定早期过滤机会来指导优化器。具体实现仅需两步：(1) 一个NFA风格的状态转换函数；(2) 一组镜像用户定义聚合函数的辅助函数。ReCAP的设计通用性强，适用于任何可推导出早期过滤的属性约束类型。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用DuckDB等标准关系型数据库配合ReCAP，可以有效将属性约束深度下推到查询计划中。在多种查询和输入图数据上的测试显示，相比当前最先进的图数据库和关系数据库，性能提升最高可达400,000倍。

Conclusion: ReCAP证明，通过适当的查询抽象，即使使用标准关系型数据库也能有效处理复杂的属性图路径查询早期过滤问题。该框架具有通用性，为各种属性约束类型提供了一致的早期过滤实现方式，为图查询处理提供了一条高效且实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Path queries are crucial for property graphs, and there is growing interest in queries that combine regular expressions over labels with constraints on property values of vertices and edges. Efficient evaluation of such general path queries requires that intermediate results be eliminated early when there is no possible completion to a full result path. Neither state-of-the-art (SOA) graph DBMS nor relational DBMS currently can do this effectively for a large class of queries. We show that this problem can be addressed by giving a relational optimizer ``a little help'' by specifying early filtering opportunities explicitly in the query. To this end, we propose ReCAP, an abstraction that greatly simplifies the implementation of early filtering techniques for any type of property constraint for which such early filtering can be derived. No matter how complex the constraint, one only needs to implement (1) an NFA-style state transition function and (2) a handful of functions that mirror those needed for user-defined aggregates. We show that when using ReCAP, a standard relational DBMS like DuckDB can effectively push property constraints deep into the query plan, beating the SOA graph and relational DBMS by a factor up to 400,000 over a variety of queries and input graphs.

</details>


### [136] [Semantic Data Processing with Holistic Data Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02655)
*Youran Sun,Sepanta Zeighami,Bhavya Chopra,Shreya Shankar,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对当前语义算子在LLM数据处理中的关键缺陷——缺乏整体数据理解能力，提出HoldUp方法。通过聚类算法识别数据集潜在结构，解决LLM数据理解悖论，实现跨记录联合处理，显著提升分类、评分和聚类任务的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义算子逐行独立处理数据记录，仅依赖用户提供的任务描述，忽视了数据上下文的重要性。导致在分类和评分等语义映射任务中，由于自然语言的模糊性，LLM无法在特定数据语境中准确解释任务，从而产生大量误分类。

Method: 1. 提出HoldUp方法，通过聚类算法识别数据集潜在结构，利用bagging思想谨慎调用LLM；2. 基于聚类结果，开发聚类基分类和评分方法，实现跨记录联合处理；3. 在15个真实数据集上验证效果。

Result: HoldUp在分类任务中最高提升33%准确率，评分和聚类任务中最高提升30%准确率。

Conclusion: 整体数据理解是提升LLM语义算子准确率的关键。HoldUp通过聚类识别数据内在结构，有效缓解了LLM长上下文导致的质量退化问题，为语义数据处理提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Semantic operators have increasingly become integrated within data systems to enable processing data using Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite significant recent effort in improving these operators, their accuracy is limited due to a critical flaw in their implementation: lack of holistic data understanding. In existing systems, semantic operators often process each data record independently using an LLM, without considering data context, only leveraging LLM's dataset-agnostic interpretation of the user-provided task. However, natural language is imprecise, so a task can only be accurately performed if it is correctly interpreted in the context of the dataset. For example, for classification and scoring tasks, which are typical semantic map tasks, the standard method of processing each record row by row yields inaccurate results in a wide range of datasets. We propose HoldUp, a new method for semantic data processing with holistic data understanding. HoldUp processes records jointly, leveraging cross-record relationships to correctly interpret the task within the data context. Enabling holistic data understanding, however, is challenging due to what we call LLM data understanding paradox: while large representative data subsets are necessary to provide context, feeding long inputs to LLMs causes quality degradation due to well-known long-context issues. To resolve this paradox, we develop a novel clustering algorithm to identify the latent structure within the dataset through judicious use of LLMs, inspired by bagging. Using this approach as a primitive, we develop novel clustering-based classification and scoring methods to perform these two tasks with high accuracy. Experiments across 15 real-world datasets show that HoldUp consistently outperforms existing solutions, providing up to 33% higher accuracy for classification and 30% higher accuracy for scoring and clustering tasks.

</details>


### [137] [Distance Comparison Operations Are Not Silver Bullets in Vector Similarity Search: A Benchmark Study on Their Merits and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02801)
*Zhuanglin Zheng,Yuxiang Zeng,Chenchen Liu,Yunzhen Chi,Binhan Yang,Yongxin Tong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 对8种DCO算法在10个数据集（最高1亿向量、12288维）和多种硬件配置（CPU/GPU）上进行综合基准测试，发现这些算法对数据维度敏感、在分布外查询下性能下降、跨硬件表现不稳定，且虽能加速索引构建和数据更新，但整体性能不稳定，最终认为DCO算法尚未准备好投入生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量相似性搜索中的距离比较操作（DCO）是关键性能瓶颈，现有DCO方法虽承诺显著加速，但其是否适用于生产向量数据库系统尚不明确，需要系统性的基准测试来验证。

Method: 综合基准测试：评估8种DCO算法，覆盖10个数据集（向量规模最高1亿、维度最高12288），测试环境包括有无SIMD的CPU以及GPU。

Result: DCO方法并非"银弹"：效率高度依赖数据维度；在分布外查询下性能严重下降；跨硬件表现不稳定；但存在被忽视的优点：可加速索引构建和数据更新。

Conclusion: 由于性能不稳定（可能比全维度扫描更慢），当前DCO算法的技术进展尚未准备好用于生产环境部署。

Abstract: Distance Comparison Operations (DCOs), which decide whether the distance between a data vector and a query is within a threshold, are a critical performance bottleneck in vector similarity search. Recent DCO methods that avoid full-dimensional distance computations promise significant speedups, but their readiness for production vector database systems remains an open question. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark of 8 DCO algorithms across 10 datasets (with up to 100M vectors and 12,288 dimensions) and diverse hardware configurations (CPUs with/without SIMD, and GPUs). Our study reveals that these methods are not silver bullets: their efficiency is highly sensitive to data dimensionality, degrades under out-of-distribution queries, and is unstable across hardware. Yet, our evaluation also demonstrates often-overlooked merits: they can accelerate index construction and data updates. Despite these benefits, their unstable performance, which can be slower than a full-dimensional scan, leads us to conclude that recent algorithmic advancements in DCO are not yet ready for production deployment.

</details>


### [138] [Unified and Efficient Approach for Multi-Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02815)
*Binhan Yang,Yuxiang Zeng,Hengxin Zhang,Zhuanglin Zheng,Yunzhen Chi,Yongxin Tong,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: MV-HNSW是首个为多向量数据设计的原生层次图索引，解决了现有方法将多向量中各token向量孤立处理而忽略相关性的问题。该方法通过设计满足对称性、基数鲁棒性和查询一致性性质的边权重函数，结合加速相似度计算算法和增强搜索策略，在7个真实数据集上实现超过90%召回率的同时将搜索延迟降低最多14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖基于单向量索引的filter-and-refine框架，将多向量对象中的token向量孤立处理而忽略其内部相关性，导致面临两难困境：激进的过滤会牺牲召回率，而保守的过滤则导致精炼阶段计算成本过高。这是由于缺乏原生多向量索引所致。

Method: 提出MV-HNSW，首个原生层次图索引，主要贡献包括：(1)设计满足对称性、基数鲁棒性和查询一致性三个必备性质的新型边权重函数；(2)开发加速多向量相似度计算算法；(3)提出增强搜索策略，能动态发现拓扑上断开但相关联的候选对象。

Result: 在7个真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，MV-HNSW达到最优搜索性能，在保持超过90%召回率的同时，搜索延迟比现有方法降低最多14.0倍。

Conclusion: MV-HNSW通过原生层次图索引设计有效解决了多向量相似度搜索中的精度-效率权衡问题，为该领域提供了新的基础架构选择。

Abstract: Multi-Vector Similarity Search is essential for fine-grained semantic retrieval in many real-world applications, offering richer representations than traditional single-vector paradigms. Due to the lack of native multi-vector index, existing methods rely on a filter-and-refine framework built upon single-vector indexes. By treating token vectors within each multi-vector object in isolation and ignoring their correlations, these methods face an inherent dilemma: aggressive filtering sacrifices recall, while conservative filtering incurs prohibitive computational cost during refinement. To address this limitation, we propose MV-HNSW, the first native hierarchical graph index designed for multi-vector data. MV-HNSW introduces a novel edge-weight function that satisfies essential properties (symmetry, cardinality robustness, and query consistency) for graph-based indexing, an accelerated multi-vector similarity computation algorithm, and an augmented search strategy that dynamically discovers topologically disconnected yet relevant candidates. Extensive experiments on seven real-world datasets show that MV-HNSW achieves state-of-the-art search performance, maintaining over 90% recall while reducing search latency by up to 14.0$\times$ compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [139] [LLM+Graph@VLDB'2025 Workshop Summary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02861)
*Yixiang Fang,Arijit Khan,Tianxing Wu,Da Yan,Shu Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 第二届LLM+Graph研讨会（与VLDB 2025联合举办）报告摘要，聚焦大语言模型与图结构数据的融合研究，涵盖算法、系统设计及实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）与图结构数据的整合已成为学术界和工业界共同关注的前沿热点，亟需系统性探索相关算法与系统解决方案。

Method: 研讨会汇集了多个研究方向的报告，涵盖：① LLM与图数据的融合算法；② 图数据管理系统；③ 图机器学习在LLM中的应用。

Result: 会议展示了该领域的关键研究方向、当前面临的挑战以及创新性解决方案。

Conclusion: LLM与图数据的交叉研究正在快速发展，成为数据管理与机器学习领域的重要趋势。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) with graph-structured data has become a pivotal and fast evolving research frontier, drawing strong interest from both academia and industry. The 2nd LLM+Graph Workshop, co-located with the 51st International Conference on Very Large Data Bases (VLDB 2025) in London, focused on advancing algorithms and systems that bridge LLMs, graph data management, and graph machine learning for practical applications. This report highlights the key research directions, challenges, and innovative solutions presented by the workshop's speakers.

</details>

### [140] [WIO: Upload-Enabled Computational Storage on CXL SSDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02442)
*Yiwei Yang,Yanpeng Hu,Yusheng Zheng,Estabon Ramos,Jianchang Su,Andi Quinn,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文针对存储与计算性能差距扩大导致数据移动成为系统瓶颈的问题，提出CXL SSD上可逆计算的解决方案。通过将I/O路径逻辑分解为可迁移的WebAssembly存储参与者，结合零拷贝迁移协议，实现了在主机与设备间的动态计算迁移，实验结果显示吞吐量提升2倍，写延迟降低3.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器速度与存储延迟之间的差距持续扩大，导致数据移动成为现代系统的主要瓶颈。虽有持久内存和计算存储设备两种创新尝试，但均因编程复杂性、生态碎片化及热功率限制等问题未能取代传统NVMe SSD。

Method: 该系统将I/O路径逻辑分解为可迁移的"存储参与者"(storage actors)，编译为WebAssembly格式；参与者通过一致的CXL.mem区域共享状态；设计热/功率感知的调度器，采用零拷贝"排空-切换"协议实现参与者在主机与设备间的动态迁移。

Result: 在基于FPGA的CXL SSD原型和两款生产级计算存储设备上的评估表明，该系统将硬热功率悬崖转化为弹性权衡，实现了最高2倍吞吐量提升和3.75倍写延迟降低，且无需修改应用程序。

Conclusion: 可逆计算模式是解决存储层计算效率问题的有效途径，通过动态迁移机制可有效应对热功率约束，为CXL SSD架构下的近数据计算提供了新思路。

Abstract: The widening gap between processor speed and storage latency has made data movement a dominant bottleneck in modern systems. Two lines of storage-layer innovation attempted to close this gap: persistent memory shortened the latency hierarchy, while computational storage devices pushed processing toward the data. Neither has displaced conventional NVMe SSDs at scale, largely due to programming complexity, ecosystem fragmentation, and thermal/power cliffs under sustained load. We argue that storage-side compute should be \emph{reversible}: computation should migrate dynamically between host and device based on runtime conditions. We present \sys, which realizes this principle on CXL SSDs by decomposing I/O-path logic into migratable \emph{storage actors} compiled to WebAssembly. Actors share state through coherent CXL.mem regions; an agility-aware scheduler migrates them via a zero-copy drain-and-switch protocol when thermal or power constraints arise. Our evaluation on an FPGA-based CXL SSD prototype and two production CSDs shows that \sys turns hard thermal cliffs into elastic trade-offs, achieving up to 2$\times$ throughput improvement and 3.75$\times$ write latency reduction without application modification.

</details>
