### [141] [Accelerating Nonlinear Time-History Analysis with Complex Constitutive Laws via Heterogeneous Memory Management: From 3D Seismic Simulation to Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02755)
*Tsuyoshi Ichimura,Kohei Fujita,Hideaki Ito,Muneo Hori,Lalith Maddegedara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Nonlinear time-history evolution problems employing high-fidelity physical models are essential in numerous scientific domains. However, these problems face a critical dual bottleneck: the immense computational cost of time-stepping and the massive memory requirements for maintaining a vast array of state variables. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework based on heterogeneous memory management for massive ensemble simulations of general nonlinear time-history problems with complex constitutive laws. Taking advantage of recent advancements in CPU-GPU interconnect bandwidth, our approach actively leverages the large capacity of host CPU memory while simultaneously maximizing the throughput of the GPU. This strategy effectively overcomes the GPU memory wall, enabling memory-intensive simulations. We evaluate the performance of the proposed method through comparisons with conventional implementations, demonstrating significant improvements in time-to-solution and energy-to-solution. Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical utility of this framework by developing a Neural Network-based surrogate model using the generated massive datasets. The results highlight the effectiveness of our approach in enabling high-fidelity 3D evaluations and its potential for broader applications in data-driven scientific discovery.

</details>


### [142] [MSAO: Adaptive Modality Sparsity-Aware Offloading with Edge-Cloud Collaboration for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02945)
*Zheming Yang,Qi Guo,Jun Wan,Jiarui Ruan,Yunqing Hu,Chang Zhao,Xiangyang Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MSAO是一个面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的自适应模态稀疏性感知卸载框架，通过边缘-云协同推理，在边缘设备上实现高效部署。核心贡献包括：提出MAS（模态激活稀疏性）指标量化各模态必要性，以及基于置信度的自适应推测执行机制，实现延迟隐藏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽然具备强大的跨模态推理能力，但在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临严峻的计算和延迟挑战，传统的边缘-云协同方案缺乏对多模态稀疏性的精细感知能力。

Method: 提出两阶段方案：(1) 轻量级异构模态感知细粒度稀疏模块，通过时空-模态联合分析计算MAS指标，以最小计算开销量化各模态必要性；(2) 自适应推测性边缘-云协同卸载机制，基于MAS分数和实时系统状态动态调度工作负载，利用置信度引导的推测执行隐藏通信延迟。

Result: 在VQAv2和MMBench基准上验证：端到端延迟降低30%，资源开销减少30%-65%，吞吐量提升1.5x-2.3x，同时保持具有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: MSAO通过模态稀疏性感知和推测执行机制，有效解决了边缘设备上MLLM部署的效率问题，为资源受限环境下的多模态推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) enable powerful cross-modal reasoning capabilities but impose substantial computational and latency burdens, posing critical challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. In this paper, we propose MSAO, an adaptive modality sparsity-aware offloading framework with edge-cloud collaboration for efficient MLLM Inference. First, a lightweight heterogeneous modality-aware via fine-grained sparsity module performs spatial-temporal-modal joint analysis to compute the Modality Activation Sparsity (MAS) metric, which quantifies the necessity of each modality with minimal computational overhead. Second, an adaptive speculative edge-cloud collaborative offloading mechanism dynamically schedules workloads between edge and cloud based on the derived MAS scores and real-time system states, leveraging confidence-guided speculative execution to hide communication latency. Extensive experiments on VQAv2 and MMBench benchmarks demonstrate that MSAO achieves a 30% reduction in end-to-end latency and 30%-65% decrease in resource overhead, while delivering a throughput improvement of 1.5x to 2.3x compared to traditional approaches, all without compromising competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [143] [TokenDance: Scaling Multi-Agent LLM Serving via Collective KV Cache Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03143)
*Zhuohang Bian,Feiyang Wu,Chengrui Zhang,Hangcheng Dong,Yun Liang,Youwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TokenDance是一个利用多智能体LLM应用中All-Gather通信模式实现集体KV Cache共享的系统，通过KV Collector在单一步骤中完成全轮次KV缓存复用，并采用Diff-Aware Storage将兄弟缓存编码为相对于主副本的块稀疏差分，实现11-17倍压缩率，显著提升系统并发能力和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM应用采用同步轮次执行模式，中心调度器收集所有智能体输出后重新分发，形成All-Gather通信模式。这种模式下每个智能体的提示词都包含相同的共享输出块，导致KV Cache大量冗余，现有的复用方法无法高效利用这一特性。

Method: TokenDance提出两个核心机制：1) KV Collector在单个集体步骤中完成全轮次的KV Cache复用，使共享块的复用成本无论智能体数量如何都只需支付一次；2) Diff-Aware Storage将兄弟缓存编码为相对于单一主副本的块稀疏差分，实现高效压缩存储。

Result: 在GenerativeAgents和AgentSociety上的评估表明：支持比vLLM前缀缓存多2.7倍的并发智能体；每个智能体的KV Cache存储减少17.5倍；在代表性工作负载上实现11-17倍压缩率；相比逐请求位置无关缓存，预填充速度提升1.9倍。

Conclusion: TokenDance通过充分利用多智能体LLM应用中的All-Gather通信模式特征，在KV Cache存储和计算效率上取得显著提升，为大规模多智能体系统提供了高效的推理基础设施。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM applications organize execution in synchronized rounds where a central scheduler gathers outputs from all agents and redistributes the combined context. This All-Gather communication pattern creates massive KV Cache redundancy, because every agent's prompt contains the same shared output blocks, yet existing reuse methods fail to exploit it efficiently. We present TokenDance, a system that scales the number of concurrent agents by exploiting the All-Gather pattern for collective KV Cache sharing. TokenDance's KV Collector performs KV Cache reuse over the full round in one collective step, so the cost of reusing a shared block is paid once regardless of agent count. Its Diff-Aware Storage encodes sibling caches as block-sparse diffs against a single master copy, achieving 11-17x compression on representative workloads. Evaluation on GenerativeAgents and AgentSociety shows that TokenDance supports up to 2.7x more concurrent agents than vLLM with prefix caching under SLO requirement, reduces per-agent KV Cache storage by up to 17.5x, and achieves up to 1.9x prefill speedup over per-request position-independent caching.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Evaluating AI-Generated Images of Cultural Artifacts with Community-Informed Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02406)
*Nari Johnson,Deepthi Sudharsan,Hamna,Samantha Dalal,Theo Holroyd,Anja Thieme,Hoda Heidari,Daniela Massiceti,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan,Cecily Morrison*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨在AI测量中如何让受影响社区参与概念系统化阶段，以"文化适当性"为案例，研究了盲人/低视力群体和印度喀拉拉邦/泰米尔纳德邦居民对文化artifact表征的期望，并尝试将其转化为多模态LLM自动测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI快速部署，但AI测量实践面临自动化与捕捉社区声音之间的张力；当前测量流程可分离为抽象概念→系统化概念→测量工具→测量值三个阶段，为社区参与提供契机。

Method: 通过三个社区案例研究（英国盲人/低视力群体、喀拉拉邦居民、泰米尔纳德邦居民），收集社区成员对文化artifact描绘的真实体验与期望，将这些经验系统化为"文化适当性"概念，并探索用多模态LLM-as-a-judge方式进行自动化测量操作化。

Result: 系统化概念反映了社区成员的生活体验及对物质文化描绘的期望，验证了社区参与在定义有效测量指标中的价值；初步探索了自动化测量操作化的可行性，但仍面临挑战。

Conclusion: 社区参与系统化阶段有助于提升测量有效性；自动化测量工具可扩大测量覆盖范围，但需平衡技术自动化与社区声音的保留；未来需解决操作化过程中的技术与伦理挑战。

Abstract: Measurement is essential to improving AI performance and mitigating harms for marginalized groups. As generative AI systems are rapidly deployed across geographies and contexts, AI measurement practices must be designed to support repeatable, automatable application across different models, datasets, and evaluation settings. But the drive to automate measurement can be in tension with the ability for measurement instruments to capture the expertise and perspectives of communities impacted by AI. Recent work advocates for breaking measurement into several key stages: first moving from an abstract concept to be measured into a precise, "systematized" concept; next operationalizing the systematized concept into a concrete measurement instrument; and finally applying the measurement instrument on data to produce measurements. This opens up an opportunity to concentrate community engagement in the systematization phase before operationalizing and applying measurement instruments. In this paper, we explore how to involve communities in systematizing the concept of "cultural appropriateness" in text-to-image models' representation of culturally significant artifacts through case studies with three communities: blind and low vision individuals residing in the UK, residents of Kerala, and residents of Tamil Nadu. Our systematized concepts reflect community members' lived experiences interacting with each artifact and how they want their material culture to be depicted, demonstrating the value of community involvement in defining valid measures. We explore how these systematized concepts can be operationalized into automated measurement instruments that could be applied using a multimodal LLM-as-a-judge approach and challenges that remain. We reflect on the benefits and limitations of such approaches.

</details>


### [145] [When simulations look right but causal effects go wrong: Large language models as behavioral simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02458)
*Zonghan Li,Feng Ji*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究评估了3个大语言模型在模拟气候变化心理干预效果上的表现。在59,508名来自62个国家参与者的数据集上，发现LLM能较好地复现态度结果（描述性拟合），但描述性拟合并不能可靠地转化为因果保真度（干预效果估计准确性）。这种描述-因果偏差在三个数据集中均存在，且对于依赖唤起内在体验的干预和行为结果更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被用于行为模拟，研究者尚不清楚LLM能否有效利用自然语言输入的人口特征和干预情境信息来推断干预效果。

Method: 研究评估了3个LLM在11个气候变化心理学干预上的表现，使用包含59,508名来自62个国家参与者的数据集，并在另外两个数据集（分别来自12和27个国家）中进行主要分析的 replication。

Result: LLM能够较为合理地复现态度结果（如气候信念和政策支持），提示优化可改善这种描述性拟合。然而，描述性拟合并不能可靠地转化为因果保真度（干预效果的准确估计），这两个准确性维度遵循不同的错误结构。这种描述-因果偏差在三个数据集中均存在，但因干预逻辑不同而有所差异：依赖唤起内在体验的干预比直接传递理由或社会线索的干预误差更大。对于行为结果这一偏差更为显著，LLM在态度-行为耦合上施加了比人类数据更强的约束。

Conclusion: 仅依赖描述性拟合可能对模拟结果产生不当的信心，导致关于干预效果的误导性结论，并掩盖对公平性重要的群体差异。描述性表现良好的国家或群体不一定因果误差更低，研究者不应仅凭描述性拟合评估LLM模拟干预效果的能力。

Abstract: Behavioral simulation is increasingly used to anticipate responses to interventions. Large language models (LLMs) enable researchers to specify population characteristics and intervention context in natural language, but it remains unclear to what extent LLMs can use these inputs to infer intervention effects. We evaluated three LLMs on 11 climate-psychology interventions using a dataset of 59,508 participants from 62 countries, and replicated the main analysis in two additional datasets (12 and 27 countries). LLMs reproduced observed patterns in attitudinal outcomes (e.g., climate beliefs and policy support) reasonably well, and prompting refinements improved this descriptive fit. However, descriptive fit did not reliably translate into causal fidelity (i.e., accurate estimates of intervention effects), and these two dimensions of accuracy followed different error structures. This descriptive-causal divergence held across the three datasets, but varied across intervention logics, with larger errors for interventions that depended on evoking internal experience than on directly conveying reasons or social cues. It was more pronounced for behavioral outcomes, where LLMs imposed stronger attitude-behavior coupling than in human data. Countries and population groups appearing well captured descriptively were not necessarily those with lower causal errors. Relying on descriptive fit alone may therefore create unwarranted confidence in simulation results, misleading conclusions about intervention effects and masking population disparities that matter for fairness.

</details>


### [146] [Generative AI Use in Entrepreneurship: An Integrative Review and an Empowerment-Entrapment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02567)
*Jackson G. Lu,Gerui Gloria Zhao,Anna Manyi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对生成式人工智能（GenAI）在创业过程中的影响进行了整合性综述，提出了"赋能-困陷"框架，将GenAI视为创业过程中的"双刃剑"，分析了其在创业机会识别、评估、资源整合以及企业创建与成长四个阶段的作用机制、核心特征、边界条件和实践启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能在创业领域的应用日益广泛，但相关研究仍较为分散，缺乏系统性整合。文章旨在填补这一空白，构建统一理论框架以理解GenAI对创业者的双重影响。

Method: 整合性文献综述方法，按照创业过程的四个阶段（机会识别与创意生成、机会评估与承诺、资源整合与动员、创业启动与成长）系统梳理GenAI的影响。

Result: 提出了"赋能-困陷"框架（Empowerment-Entrapment Framework），揭示GenAI在各阶段的"双刃剑"效应：提升创意质量但可能产生幻觉和数据偏差；增强创业自我效能感但可能导致过度自信；扩大功能广度但削弱关系嵌入性；提高生产力但滋生"工作坊"现象并侵蚀批判性思维、学习和记忆能力。

Conclusion: 识别了GenAI核心特征及其影响创业者的边界条件（如创业者元认知、领域专长和创业经验），为创业者战略性地使用GenAI同时管理其风险提供了理论指导和实践建议。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in entrepreneurship, research on its impact remains fragmented. To address this limitation, we provide an integrative review of how GenAI influences entrepreneurs at each stage of the entrepreneurial process: (1) opportunity recognition and ideation, (2) opportunity evaluation and commitment, (3) resource assembly and mobilization, and (4) venture launch and growth. Based on our review, we propose the Empowerment-Entrapment Framework to understand how GenAI can both empower and entrap entrepreneurs, highlighting GenAI's role as a double-edged sword at each stage of the entrepreneurial process. For example, GenAI may improve venture idea quality but introduce hallucinations and training data biases; boost entrepreneurial self-efficacy but heighten entrepreneurial overconfidence; increase functional breadth but decrease relational embeddedness; and boost productivity but fuel "workslop" and erode critical thinking, learning, and memory. Moreover, we identify core features of GenAI that underlie these empowering and entrapping effects. We also explore boundary conditions (e.g., entrepreneurs' metacognition, domain expertise, and entrepreneurial experience) that shape the magnitude of these effects. Beyond these theoretical contributions, our review and the Empowerment-Entrapment Framework offer practical implications for entrepreneurs seeking to use GenAI strategically throughout the entrepreneurial process while managing its risks.

</details>


### [147] [The NC State All-campus Data Science and AI Project-based Teaching and Learning (ADAPT) Model: A mechanism for interdisciplinary engagement in workforce-relevant learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02597)
*Rachel Levy,James B. Harr,David Stokes*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 北卡罗来纳州立大学创建了一个跨学科的"学院"(Academy)机构，整合全校各院系推动数据科学与AI教学、研究和社会服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据科学和AI快速发展成为独立学科和职业方向，学术机构面临挑战。现有应对措施（新证书、部门改名、新学院等）存在各自为政的问题，导致STEM学生与人文学生分离，相关教师也与原系同事疏离。

Method: 创建一种名为"Academy"的新型组织实体，突破传统学科、部门、学院、中心和研究所的界限，统整全校力量推进数据科学与AI工作。

Result: 提出了一个整合全校资源、贯穿教学-研究-社会服务三大使命的跨学科协作模式。

Conclusion: 跨学科整合模式可能是应对数据科学与AI教育挑战的有效路径。

Abstract: Academic institutions have been challenged to adapt as data science and AI have rapidly evolved into disciplines, degrees and careers. Efforts to provide students with learning experiences have led to the development of novel credentials, renamed departments, new schools and even additional colleges within universities. Generally, these approaches are siloed in some way, perhaps separating STEM students from those in the humanities or separating faculty assigned to these courses from their colleagues in their home departments. NC State University decided to take a novel approach by creating a new type of entity called an Academy that would reach across all disciplines, departments, colleges, centers and institutes to catalyze work in data science and AI in all points of the university's mission: teaching, research and engagement.

</details>


### [148] [Corporations Constitute Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02912)
*Gilad Abiri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对Anthropic于2026年发布的AI模型Claude"宪法"进行了法律与民主理论分析，指出其存在两大结构性缺陷：一是军事应用例外导致伦理约束在关键场景失效；二是其详尽性反而排除了民主讨论空间，且忽视了公司自身2023年民主制宪实验的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI治理领域缺乏民主合法性框架，需要审视企业主导的AI治理文件的规范性基础。

Method: 法律与民主理论视角的规范性分析，结合Anthropic 2023年参与式制宪实验的实证数据进行对比研究。

Result: 识别出该宪法存在两大结构性缺陷：(1)军事应用场景的规则例外；(2)过度完备性阻碍民主参与。研究发现2023年民主制宪实验产生了约50%与公司版本不同的原则，且民主版本在九个社会维度上偏见更低，但2026年版本未采纳任何相关成果。

Conclusion: AI治理存在"政治共同体赤字"——缺乏授权确定AI行为原则的民主机构。企业透明度不等于民主合法性。

Abstract: In January 2026, Anthropic published a 79-page "constitution" for its AI model Claude, the most comprehensive corporate AI governance document ever released. This Article offers the first legal and democratic-theoretic analysis of that document. Despite genuine philosophical sophistication, the constitution harbors two structural defects. First, it excludes the contexts where ethical constraints matter most: models deployed to the U.S. military operate under different rules, a gap exposed when Claude remained embedded in Palantir's Maven platform during military strikes in Iran even after a government-wide ban on Anthropic's technology. Second, its very comprehensiveness forecloses democratic contestation by resolving questions about AI values, moral status, and conscientious objection that should remain open for public deliberation. Anthropic's own 2023 experiment in participatory constitution-making found roughly 50% divergence between publicly sourced and corporate-authored principles, with the democratic version producing lower bias across nine social dimensions, yet the 2026 constitution incorporates none of those findings. I argue that AI governance suffers from a "political community deficit": the absence of any democratic body authorized to determine the principles governing AI behavior. Corporate transparency, however admirable, is not democratic legitimacy.

</details>


### [149] [Exclusive and Shared Electric Flying Taxis: Evidence on Modal Shares, Stated Reasons, and Modal Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03166)
*Nael Alsaleh,Tareq Alsaleh,Fayez Moutassem,Noura Falis,Zainab Islam*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过213份问卷调查，分析阿联酋旅客对电动飞行出租车服务的偏好，发现飞行出租车服务占总选择的22.6%，共享服务比专享服务更具吸引力，且地面出租车用户转向飞行出租车的意愿最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着城市交通拥堵加剧和可持续出行需求的增长，电动飞行出租车作为一种新兴的城市空中交通方式，其市场潜力和服务设计策略亟需深入研究。

Method: 采用陈述偏好(SP)调查法，收集213份问卷，涵盖私人车辆、公共交通、地面出租车、共享飞行出租车和专享飞行出租车五种交通方式，分析旅行时间、成本、距离、拥堵状况、出行日期和出行目的等属性的影响。

Result: 共享飞行出租车在中等距离、工作日和休闲出行中表现更好；专享服务在商务和周末出行中更受青睐；拥堵条件下飞行出租车选择率更高，距离增加则选择率下降；地面出租车用户转向共享飞行出租车的意愿最强。

Conclusion: 共享飞行出租车服务更具可持续发展潜力，建议保持经济可负担的定价策略、明确区分共享与专享服务价格、优先在拥堵走廊和中距离市场部署，以促进飞行出租车的广泛采用。

Abstract: This study examines travelers' preferences for electric flying taxi services in the United Arab Emirates (UAE) under varying travel conditions and service configurations. A stated preference (SP) survey of 213 respondents was conducted to analyze behavior across multiple transport alternatives, including private vehicles, public transport, ground taxis, and both shared and exclusive flying taxi services. The analysis considered key attributes such as travel time and cost, along with contextual factors including travel distance, congestion conditions, day of travel, and trip purpose. In addition, follow-up questions were used to capture the underlying reasons for mode choice and to assess potential modal shifts under changes in travel conditions. The results show that flying taxi services account for 22.6% of total responses, with higher shares under congested conditions and declining shares as travel distance increases. Clear differences are observed between shared and exclusive services. Shared flying taxis achieve higher modal shares and exhibit greater responsiveness to travel conditions, particularly at moderate distances, during weekdays, and for leisure trips. In contrast, exclusive flying taxis maintain lower modal shares, decline with increasing travel distance, and are more associated with business and weekend travel. The modal shift analysis further indicates that ground taxi users exhibit the highest propensity to switch to shared flying taxi services, particularly under cost increases. These findings highlight the importance of pricing and service design in promoting the adoption of shared flying taxi services as a more sustainable mobility option. In particular, maintaining affordable shared services, ensuring clear price differentiation from exclusive services, and prioritizing deployment in congested corridors and medium-distance travel markets can enhance adoption.

</details>


### [150] [Prosocial Persuasion at Scale? Large Language Models Outperform Humans in Donation Appeals Across Levels of Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03202)
*John Caffier,Olga Stavrova,Bennett Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）生成的捐赠呼吁是否与人类撰写的内容一样有效。研究通过两个预注册在线实验操纵了个性化程度（通用、个性化、虚假个性化）和内容来源（人类、LLM），结果显示LLM生成的内容产生了更多捐赠、更高参与度和更强说服力，表明LLM可能是生成促进亲社会行为内容的合适技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要关注LLM生成错误信息的风险，而LLM在亲社会说服中的作用尚待探索。该研究旨在填补这一研究空白，检验LLM是否能有效促进亲社会行为（如捐赠）。

Method: 两项预注册在线实验，采用2×3被试间设计。Study 1 (N=658) 和 Study 2 (N=642) 操纵了两个自变量：内容来源（人类 vs. LLM）和个性化程度（通用 vs. 个性化 vs. 虚假个性化）。因变量包括：参与者向慈善机构的bonus分配金额、对捐赠呼吁的参与度、以及感知说服力。

Result: 两项实验一致表明：(1) LLM生成的内容比人类撰写的内容产生更多捐赠；(2) LLM内容导致更高的参与度；(3) LLM内容被评为更具说服力。此外，个性化带来增益效应（Study 2），而虚假个性化带来惩罚效应（Study 1）。

Conclusion: LLM可能是生成鼓励亲社会行为内容的合适技术，具有实际应用价值。但需注意虚假个性化的负面影响，提示在实际应用中应确保个性化的真实性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly regarded as having the potential to generate persuasive content at scale. While previous studies have focused on the risks associated with LLM-generated misinformation, the role of LLMs in enabling prosocial persuasion is still underexplored. We investigate whether donation appeals authored by LLMs are as effective as those written by humans across degrees of personalization. Two preregistered online experiments (Study 1: N = 658; Study 2: N = 642) manipulated Personalization (generic vs. personalized vs. falsely personalized) and Content source (human vs. LLM) and presented participants with donation appeals for charities. We assessed how participants distributed their bonus money across the charities, how they engaged with the donation appeals, and how persuasive they found them. In both experiments, LLM-generated content yielded more donations, resulted in higher engagement, and was rated as more persuasive than human-authored content. There was a gain associated with personalization (Study 2) and a penalty for false personalization (Study 1). Our results suggest that LLMs may be a suitable technology for generating content that can encourage prosocial behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [What Are Adversaries Doing? Automating Tactics, Techniques, and Procedures Extraction: A Systematic Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02377)
*Mahzabin Tamanna,Shaswata Mitra,Md Erfan,Ahmed Ryan,Sudip Mittal,Laurie Williams,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项关于从非结构化文本中提取攻击策略、技术和程序（TTP）的系统文献综述，分析了80篇同行评审论文。研究发现技术级分类是主流任务范式，而战术分类和技术搜索研究不足。领域已从规则方法发展到Transformer架构（Bert、SecureBERT、RoBERTa），近期开始探索LLM方法。主要局限包括单标签分类、评估设置单一、数据集狭窄及可复现性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对手持续演化其战术、技术和程序（TTP）以逃避检测，而现有研究在提取目标、数据集、建模方法和评估实践上差异巨大，难以理解该领域的研究全景。防御者需要持续更新对对手行为的理解，亟需系统性梳理该领域现状。

Method: 系统文献综述方法，从关键维度分析80篇同行评审论文：提取目的、数据来源、数据集构建、建模方法、评估指标及工件可用性。

Result: 技术级分类是主导任务形式，战术分类和技术搜索研究不足。领域已从规则方法和传统机器学习演进至Transformer架构（如BERT、SecureBERT、RoBERTa），近期开始探索LLM方法（提示、RAG、微调），但应用仍处于萌芽阶段。

Conclusion: 该领域存在重要局限：多数研究依赖单标签分类、评估设置单一、数据集狭窄（限制跨领域泛化）；可复现性受限于专有数据集、代码发布有限和语料库受限。未来应关注多标签分类、多样化评估设置、跨领域泛化及提高研究可复现性。

Abstract: Adversaries continuously evolve their tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) to achieve their objectives while evading detection, requiring defenders to continually update their understanding of adversary behavior. Prior research has proposed automated extraction of TTP-related intelligence from unstructured text and mapping it to structured knowledge bases, such as MITRE ATT&CK. However, existing work varies widely in extraction objectives, datasets, modeling approaches, and evaluation practices, making it difficult to understand the research landscape. The goal of this study is to aid security researchers in understanding the state of the art in extracting attack tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from unstructured text by analyzing relevant literature. We systematically analyze 80 peer-reviewed studies across key dimensions: extraction purposes, data sources, dataset construction, modeling approaches, evaluation metrics, and artifact availability. Our analysis reveals several dominant trends. Technique-level classification remains the dominant task formulation, while tactic classification and technique searching are underexplored. The field has progressed from rule-based and traditional machine learning to transformer-based architectures (e.g., BERT, SecureBERT, RoBERTa), with recent studies exploring LLM-based approaches including prompting, retrieval-augmented generation, and fine-tuning, though adoption remains emergent. Despite these advances, important limitations persist: many studies rely on single-label classification, limited evaluation settings, and narrow datasets, constraining cross-domain generalization. Reproducibility is further hindered by proprietary datasets, limited code releases, and restricted corpora.

</details>


### [152] [Ambig-IaC: Multi-level Disambiguation for Interactive Cloud Infrastructure-as-Code Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02382)
*Zhenning Yang,Kaden Gruizenga,Tongyuan Miao,Patrick Tser Jern Kon,Hui Guan,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Ambig-IaC框架，通过识别IaC配置中的结构化歧义（资源、拓扑、属性三个层级），利用分歧驱动生成多样化候选规范，并主动生成澄清问题以逐步缩小配置空间。该方法在300个验证任务上，相比最强基线模型在结构评估和属性评估上分别提升18.4%和25.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云基础设施的规模和复杂性使IaC成为必需，但LLM生成IaC配置时面临用户需求描述不完整的挑战。传统代码生成的迭代修复方法不适用于IaC（无法廉价执行或迭代修复），迫使LLM几乎处于一次性生成模式。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的"分歧驱动"框架：1）生成多样化候选规格；2）跨三个层级轴（资源、拓扑、属性）识别结构分歧；3）按信息量排序；4）生成有针对性的澄清问题逐步缩小配置空间。引入Ambig-IaC基准测试（300个带歧义提示的验证任务）和基于图编辑距离与嵌入相似度的评估框架。

Result: 方法优于最强基线模型，在结构评估上相对提升18.4%，在属性评估上相对提升25.4%。

Conclusion: 通过利用IaC歧义的可组合结构特性，无需额外训练即可有效识别和处理IaC配置中的歧义问题，生成的澄清问题能有效引导用户完善需求描述。

Abstract: The scale and complexity of modern cloud infrastructure have made Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) essential for managing deployments. While large Language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used to generate IaC configurations from natural language, user requests are often underspecified. Unlike traditional code generation, IaC configurations cannot be executed cheaply or iteratively repaired, forcing the LLMs into an almost one-shot regime. We observe that ambiguity in IaC exhibits a tractable compositional structure: configurations decompose into three hierarchical axes (resources, topology, attributes) where higher-level decisions constrain lower-level ones. We propose a training-free, disagreement-driven framework that generates diverse candidate specifications, identifies structural disagreements across these axes, ranks them by informativeness, and produces targeted clarification questions that progressively narrow the configuration space. We introduce \textsc{Ambig-IaC}, a benchmark of 300 validated IaC tasks with ambiguous prompts, and an evaluation framework based on graph edit distance and embedding similarity. Our method outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving relative improvements of +18.4\% and +25.4\% on structure and attribute evaluations, respectively.

</details>


### [153] [Improving MPI Error Detection and Repair with Large Language Models and Bug References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02398)
*Scott Piersall,Yang Gao,Shenyang Liu,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对MPI程序的bug检测与修复问题，提出结合少样本学习（FSL）、思维链推理（CoT）和检索增强生成（RAG）技术来增强大语言模型的能力，将错误检测准确率从44%提升至77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI是高性能计算的基础技术，广泛应用于PyTorch、TensorFlow等框架的分布式训练。然而MPI程序因进程间复杂的交互和消息传递/同步机制而难以维护。直接应用LLM效果欠佳（仅44%准确率），主要原因是大模型缺乏MPI程序的正确/错误用法知识。

Method: 设计一套bug检测与修复技术，融合 Few-Shot Learning（FSL）提供示例引导、Chain-of-Thought（CoT）实现推理过程可视化、以及 Retrieval Augmented Generation（RAG）检索相关bug知识，增强LLM对MPI错误的检测和修复能力。

Result: 通过增强技术，MPI错误检测准确率从44%显著提升至77%。实验表明该bug引用技术具有良好的泛化能力，可推广至其他大语言模型。

Conclusion: FSL、CoT和RAG的组合可有效弥补LLM在MPI领域知识上的不足，为自动化程序调试提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Message Passing Interface (MPI) is a foundational technology in high-performance computing (HPC), widely used for large-scale simulations and distributed training (e.g., in machine learning frameworks such as PyTorch and TensorFlow). However, maintaining MPI programs remains challenging due to their complex interplay among processes and the intricacies of message passing and synchronization. With the advancement of large language models like ChatGPT, it is tempting to adopt such technology for automated error detection and repair. Yet, our studies reveal that directly applying large language models (LLMs) yields suboptimal results, largely because these models lack essential knowledge about correct and incorrect usage, particularly the bugs found in MPI programs. In this paper, we design a bug detection and repair technique alongside Few-Shot Learning (FSL), Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, and Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) techniques in LLMs to enhance the large language model's ability to detect and repair errors. Surprisingly, such enhancements lead to a significant improvement, from 44% to 77%, in error detection accuracy compared to baseline methods that use ChatGPT directly. Additionally, our experiments demonstrate our bug referencing technique generalizes well to other large language models.

</details>


### [154] [A Synthesis Method of Safe Rust Code Based on Pushdown Colored Petri Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02399)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Safe Rust guarantees memory safety through strict compile-time constraints: ownership can be transferred, borrowing can temporarily guarantee either shared read-only or exclusive write access, and ownership and borrowing are scoped by lifetime. Automatically synthesizing correct and safe Rust code is challenging, as the generated code must not only satisfy ownership, borrowing, and lifetime constraints, but also meet type and interface requirements at compile time. This work proposes a synthesis method based on our newly defined Pushdown Colored Petri Net (PCPN) that models these compilation constraints directly from public API signatures to synthesize valid call sequences. Token colors encode dynamic resource states together with a scope level indicating the lifetime region in which a borrow is valid. The pushdown stack tracks the entering or leaving of lifetime parameter via pushing and popping tokens. A transition is enabled only when type matching and interface obligations both hold and the required resource states are available. Based on the bisimulation theory, we prove that the enabling and firing rules of PCPN are consistent with the compile-time check of these three constraints. We develop an automatic synthesis tool based on PCPN and the experimental results show that the synthesized codes are all correct.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond Resolution Rates: Behavioral Drivers of Coding Agent Success and Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02547)
*Tural Mehtiyev,Wesley Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对编码智能体进行了大规模实证研究，分析了19个智能体在500个任务上的9,374条轨迹，发现即使是最简单的补丁也无法被所有智能体解决，根本原因在于架构推理和领域知识的缺失；轨迹结构（如先收集上下文再编辑、投入验证）是成功的关键预测因素；LLM是性能和行为的主要驱动因素，而非框架设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体代表了自动化软件工程的新范式，但仍存在严重局限——即便排名靠前的LLM智能体在基准测试中仍有超过20%的失败率。然而，学界对智能体失败的根本原因和行为机制缺乏系统理解。

Method: 大规模实证研究，分析9,374条轨迹，涵盖8个编码智能体框架和14个LLM，在500个任务上进行测试，围绕三个研究问题展开：1）失败原因；2）成功与失败的行为模式差异；3）LLM能力与框架设计的解耦。

Result: 发现一：补丁复杂度不能解释任务难度，12个从未被解决的任务只需简单补丁但因架构推理和领域知识缺失而失败；发现二：轨迹长度与失败的相关性是伪相关，控制任务难度后轨迹结构才是区分因素——在编辑前收集上下文和投入验证的策略与成功强相关；发现三：LLM是性能和行为的主要驱动因素，同一LLM的智能体在更多任务上达成一致，且框架性能差距随LLM代际改进而缩小。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的失败主要源于LLM在架构推理和领域知识上的根本性缺失，而非任务复杂度；成功的关键在于智能体行为策略（上下文收集和验证），这些策略由智能体自身决定而非任务适应性；未来改进应聚焦于LLM能力的提升，框架设计的影响随LLM增强而递减。

Abstract: Coding agents represent a new paradigm in automated software engineering, combining the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) with tool-augmented interaction loops. However, coding agents still have severe limitations. Top-ranked LLM-based coding agents still fail on over 20% of benchmarked problems. Yet, we lack a systematic understanding of why (i.e., the causes) agents fail, and how failure unfolds behaviorally. We present a large-scale empirical study analyzing 9,374 trajectories from 19 agents (8 coding agent frameworks, 14 LLMs) on 500 tasks. We organize our analysis around three research questions. First, we investigate why agents fail on specific tasks and find that patch complexity alone does not explain difficulty: 12 never-solved tasks require only simple patches and were considered easy by human annotators, yet all agents fail due to gaps in architectural reasoning and domain knowledge. Second, we examine how behavioral patterns differentiate success from failure. The widely reported correlation between trajectory length and failure reverses direction once task difficulty is controlled, revealing it as a confound. Instead, trajectory structure discriminates consistently: agents that gather context before editing and invest in validation succeed more often, and these strategies are agent-determined rather than task-adaptive. Third, we disentangle LLM capability from framework design and find that the LLM is the primary driver of both outcome and behavior: agents sharing the same LLM agree on far more tasks than agents sharing the same framework, and the framework performance gap shrinks with each generation of LLM improvement. Framework prompts do influence agent tactics, but this influence diminishes with stronger LLMs.

</details>


### [156] [Runtime Execution Traces Guided Automated Program Repair with Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02647)
*Jiaqing Wu,Tong Wu,Manqing Zhang,Yunwei Dong,Bo Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceRepair是一个多智能体框架，通过将运行时事实作为共享约束来验证和迭代改进候选补丁，从而解决当前LLM驱动的自动化程序修复(APR)方法在处理复杂逻辑错误时的静态局限性。在Defects4J基准上正确修复了392个缺陷，显著优于现有方法，并在新构建的近期bug数据集上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR方法主要依赖静态信息（源代码和基本测试输出），难以准确捕获复杂运行时行为和动态数据依赖。单LLM独立解释运行时证据容易过度拟合特定假设，产生"巧合通过测试"而非真正正确逻辑的补丁。

Method: 提出多智能体框架TraceRepair：1) Probe Agent捕获关键变量的执行快照形成客观修复基础；2) Specialized Agents委员会交叉验证候选补丁以暴露不一致性并迭代改进。核心思想：运行时证据应作为客观约束而非额外输入。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上正确修复392个缺陷，显著优于现有LLM-based方法。在新构建的近期bug数据集上验证了泛化能力，证明性能提升来自动态推理而非记忆。

Conclusion: 通过将运行时证据转化为客观共享约束，结合多智能体交叉验证机制，TraceRepair能有效解决复杂逻辑错误的自动化修复问题，展现出良好的泛化性能和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) struggles with complex logic errors and silent failures. Current LLM-based APR methods are mostly static, relying on source code and basic test outputs, which fail to accurately capture complex runtime behaviors and dynamic data dependencies. While incorporating runtime evidence like execution traces exposes concrete state transitions, a single LLM interpreting this in isolation often overfits to specific hypotheses, producing patches that satisfy tests by coincidence rather than correct logic. Therefore, runtime evidence should act as objective constraints rather than mere additional input. We propose TraceRepair, a multi-agent framework that leverages runtime facts as shared constraints for patch validation. A probe agent captures execution snapshots of critical variables to form an objective repair basis. Meanwhile, a committee of specialized agents cross-verifies candidate patches to expose inconsistencies and iteratively refine them. Evaluated on the Defects4J benchmark, TraceRepair correctly fixes 392 defects, substantially outperforming existing LLM-based approaches. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved efficiency and strong generalization on a newly constructed dataset of recent bugs, confirming that performance gains arise from dynamic reasoning rather than memorization.

</details>


### [157] [GBQA: A Game Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs as Quality Assurance Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02648)
*Shufan Jiang,Chios Chen,Zhiyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了GBQA基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在游戏开发领域自主发现软件bug的能力。基准包含30款游戏和124个人类验证的bug，最佳模型Claude-4.6-Opus仅能发现48.39%的bug，表明LLM自主bug发现仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主发现bug是现代软件开发中的重大挑战。相比代码生成，动态运行时环境的复杂性使bug发现对LLM来说更加困难。现有缺乏标准化的评估基准来衡量LLM在这一任务上的能力。

Method: 1. 构建GBQA基准测试：30款游戏、124个人类验证的bug，分为三个难度级别
2. 使用多智能体系统开发游戏并注入bug，通过人工专家确保正确性
3. 提供基线交互智能体：配备多轮ReAct循环和记忆机制，支持长时域游戏环境探索
4. 在前沿LLM上进行广泛实验

Result: 最佳模型Claude-4.6-Opus（思考模式）仅能识别48.39%的已验证bug，自主bug发现仍然极具挑战性。

Conclusion: GBQA提供了合适的测试平台和评估标准，在该基准上的进一步进展将有助于缩小自主软件工程领域的差距。

Abstract: The autonomous discovery of bugs remains a significant challenge in modern software development. Compared to code generation, the complexity of dynamic runtime environments makes bug discovery considerably harder for large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we take game development as a representative domain and introduce the Game Benchmark for Quality Assurance (GBQA), a benchmark containing 30 games and 124 human-verified bugs across three difficulty levels, to evaluate whether LLMs can autonomously detect software bugs. The benchmark is constructed using a multi-agent system that develops games and injects bugs in a scalable manner, with human experts in the loop to ensure correctness. Moreover, we provide a baseline interactive agent equipped with a multi-round ReAct loop and a memory mechanism, enabling long-horizon exploration of game environments for bug detection across different LLMs. Extensive experiments on frontier LLMs demonstrate that autonomous bug discovery remains highly challenging: the best-performing model, Claude-4.6-Opus in thinking mode, identifies only 48.39% of the verified bugs. We believe GBQA provides an adequate testbed and evaluation criterion, and that further progress on it will help close the gap in autonomous software engineering.

</details>


### [158] [AgentSZZ: Teaching the LLM Agent to Play Detective with Bug-Inducing Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02665)
*Yunbo Lyu,Jieke Shi,Hong Jin Kang,Ratnadira Widyasari,Junda He,Yuqing Niu,Chengran Yang,Junkai Chen,Zhou Yang,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentSZZ框架，利用LLM驱动的智能体和ReAct风格推理解决传统SZZ算法在跨文件和幽灵缺陷场景中的局限性问题，在三个数据集上实现F1分数最高27.2%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SZZ算法依赖git blame追踪文件内行级变更，无法处理幽灵缺陷和跨文件缺陷，导致约1/4的bug-inducing提交本质不可追溯；现有方法采用固定流水线，缺乏交互式推理和探索能力。

Method: AgentSZZ采用基于智能体的框架，集成任务特定工具和领域知识，通过ReAct风格循环实现自适应因果追踪；结构化压缩模块减少冗余上下文同时保留关键证据。

Result: AgentSZZ在三个数据集上全面超越SOTA方法：整体F1提升最高27.2%；跨文件场景召回率提升300%；幽灵缺陷场景召回率提升60%；上下文压缩工具减少超过30% token消耗且影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 任务特定工具和领域知识对性能提升至关重要；基于智能体的交互式推理框架能有效解决传统SZZ算法的结构性局限，为缺陷定位研究开辟新方向。

Abstract: The SZZ algorithm is the dominant technique for identifying bug-inducing commits and underpins many software engineering tasks, such as defect prediction and vulnerability analysis. Despite numerous variants, including recent LLM-based approaches, performance remains limited on developer-annotated datasets (e.g., recall of 0.552 on the Linux kernel). A key limitation is the reliance on git blame, which traces line-level changes within the same file, failing in common scenarios such as ghost and cross-file cases-making nearly one-quarter of bug-inducing commits inherently untraceable. Moreover, current approaches follow fixed pipelines that restrict iterative reasoning and exploration, unlike developers who investigate bugs through an interactive, multi-tool process. To address these challenges, we propose AgentSZZ, an agent-based framework that leverages LLM-driven agents to explore repositories and identify bug-inducing commits. Unlike prior methods, AgentSZZ integrates task-specific tools, domain knowledge, and a ReAct-style loop to enable adaptive and causal tracing of bugs. A structured compression module further improves efficiency by reducing redundant context while preserving key evidence. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that AgentSZZ consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SZZ algorithms across all settings, achieving F1-score gains of up to 27.2% over prior LLM-based approaches. The improvements are especially pronounced in challenging scenarios such as cross-file and ghost commits, with recall gains of up to 300% and 60%, respectively. Ablation studies show that task-specific tools and domain knowledge are critical, while compression tool outputs reduce token consumption by over 30% with negligible impact. The replication package is available.

</details>


### [159] [TypePro: Boosting LLM-Based Type Inference via Inter-Procedural Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02702)
*Teyu Lin,Minghao Fan,Huaxun Huang,Zhirong Shen,Rongxin Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TypePro方法，利用大语言模型(LLM)进行动态语言类型推断。通过过程间代码切片补充上下文信息，并基于切片中的数据类型结构信息提出候选复杂类型，解决LLM领域知识不足的问题。在Python和TypeScript数据集上分别达到88.9%和86.6%的Top-1精确匹配率，较最佳基线方法分别提升7.1和10.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态语言（如Python、JavaScript）的类型灵活性虽然简化了编程，但同时也导致了类型相关错误的增加和编译时类型推断的开销。现有的类型推断方法（包括静态分析、机器学习和LLM方法）仅关注与类型推断相关的变量的直接依赖关系，导致上下文信息不完整，从而影响类型推断的准确性。

Method: TypePro方法包含两个核心创新点：（1）通过过程间代码切片（inter-procedural code slicing）补充上下文信息，获取更全面的代码依赖关系；（2）基于切片中隐含的数据类型结构信息，提出一组候选复杂类型（candidate complex types），以弥补LLM领域知识的不足。

Result: 在ManyTypes4Py数据集上达到88.9%的Top-1精确匹配率，在ManyTypes4TypeScript数据集上达到86.6%的Top-1精确匹配率。

Conclusion: TypePro通过过程间代码切片和候选复杂类型策略，有效解决了现有方法中上下文信息不完整和LLM领域知识不足的问题。该方法在两种动态语言上的实验结果表明其具有良好的有效性和鲁棒性，Top-1精确匹配率较最佳基线方法分别提升7.1和10.3个百分点。

Abstract: Dynamic languages (such as Python and JavaScript) offer flexibility and simplified type handling for programming, but this can also lead to an increase in type-related errors and additional overhead for compile-time type inference. As a result, type inference for dynamic languages has become a popular research area. Existing approaches typically achieve type inference through static analysis, machine learning, or large language models (LLMs). However, current work only focuses on the direct dependencies of variables related to type inference as the context, resulting in incomplete contextual information and thus affecting the accuracy of type inference. To address this issue, this paper proposes a method called TypePro, which leverages LLMs for type inference in dynamic languages. TypePro supplements contextual information by conducting inter-procedural code slicing. Then, TypePro proposes a set of candidate complex types based on the structural information of data types implied in the slices, thereby addressing the lack of domain knowledge of LLMs. We conducted experiments on the ManyTypes4Py and ManyTypes4TypeScript datasets, achieving Top-1 exact match (EM) rates of 88.9% and 86.6%, respectively. Notably, TypePro improves the Top-1 Exact Match by 7.1 and 10.3 percentage points over the second-best approach, showing the effectiveness and robustness of TypePro.

</details>


### [160] [IndustryCode: A Benchmark for Industry Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02729)
*Puyu Zeng,Zhaoxi Wang,Zhixu Duan,Liang Feng,Shaobo Wang,Cunxiang Wang,Jinghang Wang,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IndustryCode是一个综合性基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在跨工业领域和编程语言场景下的代码生成能力，填补了现有基准测试在多领域泛化能力评估方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准测试主要局限于单一领域和语言，无法有效评估模型在真实工业应用中的泛化能力，也无法反映复杂工业场景下的编程水平需求。

Method: 构建了IndustryCode基准测试，包含579个子问题和125个主要工业挑战，每个问题配有严格的问题描述和测试用例，覆盖金融、自动化、航空航天、遥感等领域，涉及MATLAB、Python、C++、Stata等多种编程语言。

Result: 评估结果显示，表现最佳的模型Claude 4.5 Opus在子问题上的总体准确率为68.1%，在主要问题上的准确率为42.5%。

Conclusion: IndustryCode为评估LLM在工业场景中的代码生成能力提供了首个全面的多领域、多语言基准，揭示了当前模型仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Code generation and comprehension by Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as core drivers of industrial intelligence and decision optimization, finding widespread application in fields such as finance, automation, and aerospace. Although recent advancements have demonstrated the remarkable potential of LLMs in general code generation, existing benchmarks are mainly confined to single domains and languages. Consequently, they fail to effectively evaluate the generalization capabilities required for real-world industrial applications or to reflect the coding proficiency demanded by complex industrial scenarios. To bridge this gap, we introduce IndustryCode, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to span multiple industrial domains and programming languages. IndustryCode comprises 579 sub-problems derived from 125 primary industrial challenges, accompanied by rigorous problem descriptions and test cases. It covers a wide range of fields, including finance, automation, aerospace, and remote sensing-and incorporates diverse programming languages such as MATLAB, Python, C++, and Stata. In our evaluation, the top-performing model, Claude 4.5 Opus, achieved an overall accuracy of 68.1% on sub-problems and 42.5% main problems. The benchmark dataset and automated evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [Sustainability Analysis of Prompt Strategies for SLM-based Automated Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02761)
*Pragati Kumari,Novarun Deb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次系统评估Small Language Models (SLMs) 中提示工程策略对自动化测试生成的可持续性影响。研究发现提示策略对可持续性指标的影响甚至超过模型选择本身，推理密集型策略(CoT、Self-Consistency)覆盖率更高但环境成本显著，而简单策略(Zero-Shot、ReAct)则以较低环境代价提供竞争性测试质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动测试的可持续性研究主要关注大型语言模型，忽视了提示工程策略的影响。提示设计直接影响推理行为、执行成本和资源利用效率，这对SLMs尤为重要，因为SLMs在边缘设备和资源受限环境中应用广泛，但提示设计策略的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 受控实验环境下，对3个开源SLMs评估7种提示策略。采用多维度联合评估方法：执行时间、Token使用量、能源消耗、碳排放量，以及通过覆盖率分析评估生成的测试脚本质量。

Result: 提示策略对可持续性指标有显著且独立的影响，甚至超过模型选择的影响。Chain of Thought和Self-Consistency等推理密集型策略获得更高覆盖率，但执行时间、能源消耗和碳排放显著增加。Zero-Shot和ReAct等简单策略以明显更低的环境成本提供竞争性的测试质量。Least-to-Most和Program of Thought提供平衡的权衡。

Conclusion: 提示工程策略是影响SLMs自动化测试可持续性的关键因素。研究建议：根据具体场景选择提示策略——追求高质量测试可选择推理密集型策略，重视环保可持续性则选择简单策略。提示策略优化应成为AI测试可持续性研究的重要方向。

Abstract: The growing adoption of prompt-based automation in software testing raises important issues regarding its computational and environmental sustainability. Existing sustainability studies in AI-driven testing primarily focus on large language models, leaving the impact of prompt engineering strategies largely unexplored - particularly in the context of Small Language Models (SLMs). This gap is critical, as prompt design directly influences inference behavior, execution cost, and resource utilization, even when model size is fixed. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first systematic sustainability evaluation of prompt engineering strategies for automated test generation using SLMs. We analyze seven prompt strategies across three open-source SLMs under a controlled experimental setup. Our evaluation jointly considers execution time, token usage, energy consumption, carbon emissions, and coverage test quality, the latter assessed through coverage analysis of the generated test scripts. The results show that prompt strategies have a substantial and independent impact on sustainability outcomes, often outweighing the effect of model choice. Reasoning intensive strategies such as Chain of Thought and Self-Consistency achieve higher coverage but incur significantly higher execution time, energy consumption, and carbon emissions. In contrast, simpler strategies such as Zero-Shot and ReAct deliver competitive coverage test quality with markedly lower environmental cost, while Least-to-Most and Program of Thought offer balanced trade-offs.

</details>


### [162] [Evaluating the Environmental Impact of using SLMs and Prompt Engineering for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02776)
*Md Afif Al Mamun,Sayan Nath,Gias Uddin,Novarun Deb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次系统研究SLM代码生成中提示工程策略对准确性与可持续性的影响，发现Chain-of-Thought在能效与性能间达到近最优平衡，而多采样策略成本收益比不佳，电网碳强度是部署时排放的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助编程从云端LLM转向本地SLM部署，AI的环境足迹已去中心化。提示工程策略（如CoT、ReAct）虽可优化代码生成，但其能源消耗和碳排放对开发者而言仍不可见，缺乏系统性实证研究。

Method: 对11个开源模型（1B-34B参数）在HumanEval+和MBPP+上评估6种提示工程策略，同时测量Pass@1准确率、能耗(kWh)、碳排放(kgCO₂eq)和推理延迟。

Result: 可持续性与准确性常可解耦——显著的环境优化无需牺牲性能。Chain-of-Thought作为更简单的技术，能在推理能力与能效间达到近最优平衡；多采样策略则往往得不偿失。电网碳强度是部署时排放的主导因素。

Conclusion: 本研究为"绿色提示工程"提供量化基础，使开发者能在高性能代码生成与生态责任间做出知情选择。

Abstract: The shift from cloud-hosted Large Language Models (LLMs) to locally deployed open-source Small Language Models (SLMs) has democratized AI-assisted coding; however, it has also decentralized the environmental footprint of AI. While prompting strategies - such as Chain-of-Thought and ReAct - serve as external mechanisms for optimizing code generation without modifying model parameters, their impact on energy consumption and carbon emissions remains largely invisible to developers. This paper presents the first systematic empirical study investigating how different prompt engineering strategies in SLM-based code generation impact code generation accuracy alongside sustainability factors. We evaluate six prominent prompting strategies across 11 open-source models (ranging from 1B to 34B parameters) using the HumanEval+ and MBPP+ benchmarks. By measuring Pass@1 accuracy alongside energy (kWh), carbon emissions (kgCO2eq), and inference latency, we reveal that sustainability often decouples from accuracy, allowing significant environmental optimizations without sacrificing performance. Our findings indicate that Chain-of-Thought, being a simpler prompting technique, can provide a near-optimal balance between reasoning capability and energy efficiency. Conversely, multi-sampling strategies often incur disproportionate costs for marginal gains. Finally, we identify grid carbon intensity as the dominant factor in deployment-time emissions, highlighting the need for practitioners to consider regional energy profiles. This work provides a quantitative foundation for "green" prompt engineering, enabling developers to align high-performance code generation with ecological responsibility.

</details>


### [163] [Dependency-Guided Repository-Level C-to-Rust Translation with Reinforcement Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02852)
*Jia Feng,Wenjie Gan,Cuiyun Gao,Chaozheng Wang,Feng Luo,Xin Xia,Ge Li,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DepTrans是一个结合模型能力增强与结构化推理的C-to-Rust自动迁移框架，通过强化对齐句法训练和依赖引导的迭代优化技术，解决了现有LLM方法在跨文件依赖处理、代码正确性验证和训练数据规模方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的C-to-Rust迁移方法难以应对多样化的C语言惯用法，生成的Rust代码僵硬且不符合Rust idiom；现有LLM方法在处理跨文件依赖时要么忽略要么将整个文件作为上下文，且缺乏大规模C-Rust并行数据支持。

Method: 提出DepTrans框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) Reinforcement-Aligned Syntax Training：结合多任务微调与反馈驱动的强化学习提升生成质量；(2) Dependency-Guided Iterative Refinement：捕获细粒度跨文件依赖并迭代优化生成的Rust代码。构建85k训练样本和145个仓库级测试实例的基准数据集。

Result: DepTrans达到60.7%的编译成功率和43.5%的计算准确率，分别比最强基线提升22.8和17.3个百分点；成功构建15个工业C项目中的7个。

Conclusion: DepTrans框架通过结构化推理和依赖建模显著提升了LLM驱动的代码迁移质量，验证了其在实际工业场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Automating C-to-Rust migration is critical for improving software security without sacrificing performance. Traditional rule-based methods struggle with diverse C idioms, often producing rigid and unidiomatic Rust code. Large Language Models (LLMs), trained on massive code corpora, offer a promising alternative by leveraging cross-language generalization to generate more idiomatic and maintainable Rust code. However, several challenges remain. First, existing LLM-based approaches fail to handle cross-file dependencies effectively, either ignoring them or including entire files as context, which limits accurate dependency modeling. Second, complex dependencies and structured inputs and outputs make it difficult to verify syntactic correctness and functional equivalence at the repository level. Third, the lack of large-scale C-Rust parallel data constrains model performance.
  We propose DepTrans, a framework that combines model capability enhancement with structured inference. DepTrans introduces Reinforcement-Aligned Syntax Training to improve generation quality through multi-task fine-tuning and feedback-driven reinforcement learning. It further applies Dependency-Guided Iterative Refinement to capture fine-grained cross-file dependencies and iteratively refine generated Rust code. We construct a dataset of 85k training samples and a benchmark of 145 repository-level instances. Experiments show that DepTrans achieves a 60.7 percent compilation success rate and 43.5 percent computational accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 22.8 and 17.3 percentage points. It also successfully builds 7 of 15 industrial C projects, demonstrating its practical potential.

</details>


### [164] [BugForge: Constructing and Utilizing DBMS Bug Repository to Enhance DBMS Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03024)
*Dawei Li,Qifan Liu,Yuxiao Guo,Jie Liang,Zhiyong Wu,Chi Zhang,Jingzhou Fu,Haogang Mao,Zhenyu Guan,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BugForge是一个从异构Bug报告中构建标准化DBMS Bug仓库并生成高质量测试用例的框架。该系统针对PostgreSQL、MySQL、MariaDB和MonetDB四个数据库实现，整合了37,632个Bug报告（跨度达28年），并发现了35个先前未知的Bug（其中22个已被开发者确认）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DBMS是复杂系统，易产生导致系统故障或数据完整性问题的Bug。Bug报告包含有价值的测试输入和Bug触发线索，有助于探索罕见执行路径和暴露关键Bug行为。然而，Bug报告的异构性以及内容的不完整或不准确性使得构建统一仓库并将报告转化为高质量测试用例面临挑战。

Method: BugForge采用三阶段处理方法：(1) 渐进式收集Bug报告；(2) 通过语法感知处理和输入自适应的原始PoC提取构建Bug仓库；(3) 通过语义引导的适配将数据精化为高质量测试用例。这些测试用例可支持DBMS模糊测试、回归测试和跨DBMS Bug发现。

Result: 对PostgreSQL、MySQL、MariaDB和MonetDB四个DBMS实现了BugForge，共整合37,632个Bug报告（时间跨度达28年）。基于该仓库，发现了35个先前未知的Bug，其中22个已被开发者确认。

Conclusion: 构建和利用Bug仓库对于DBMS测试具有重要价值，能够系统化组织Bug相关数据，支持代码改进，并为自动化测试提供有力支持。

Abstract: DBMSs are complex systems prone to bugs that may lead to system failures or compromise data integrity. Establishing unified DBMS bug repositories is crucial for systematically organizing bug-related data, enabling code improvement, and supporting automated testing. In particular, bug reports often contain valuable test inputs and bug-triggering clues that help explore rare execution paths and expose critical buggy behavior, thereby guiding automated DBMS testing. However, the heterogeneity of bug reports, along with their incomplete or inaccurate content, makes it challenging to build unified repositories and convert them into high-quality test cases.
  In this paper, we propose BugForge, a framework that constructs standardized DBMS bug repositories and leverages them to generate high-quality test cases to enhance DBMS testing. Specifically, BugForge progressively collects bug reports, then employs syntax-aware processing and input-adaptive raw PoC extraction to construct a DBMS bug repository. The repository stores structured bug-related data, including bug metadata and raw PoCs that entail potential bug-triggering semantics. These data are further refined into high-quality test cases through semantic-guided adaptation, thereby enabling enhanced DBMS testing methods, including DBMS fuzzing, regression testing, and cross-DBMS bug discovery. We implemented BugForge for PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB, and MonetDB, totally integrated 37,632 bug reports spanning up to 28 years. Based on the repository, BugForge uncovered 35 previously unknown bugs with 22 confirmed by developers, demonstrating the value of constructing and utilizing bug repositories for DBMS testing.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Isolated Tasks: A Framework for Evaluating Coding Agents on Sequential Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03035)
*KN Ajay Shastry,Ganesh Senrayan,Shrey Satapara,Pranoy Panda,Chaitanya Devaguptapu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-STEPS是一个用于评估编程智能体长期任务能力的新数据集，通过模拟真实开发者工作流（对话式迭代编程和PRD文档驱动编程）来弥补现有数据集仅评估孤立PR的不足。研究发现现有评估方法高估成功率约20个百分点，且成功解决问题的智能体仍会产生更高的代码复杂度和技术债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程智能体评估数据集存在关键缺陷：仅评估孤立、单一PR任务，无法反映真实软件开发中代码累积、技术债务累积和测试套件增长的实际情况。这种脱离实际场景的评估可能导致对智能体能力的过度乐观估计。

Method: 提出了一个自动化编程任务生成框架，构建SWE-STEPS数据集，通过两种贴近实际的设置评估智能体：1）对话式编程（迭代请求）；2）基于项目需求文档(PRD)的单次编程。关键创新是评估跨依赖PR链的性能，支持顺序执行、回归验证和长期代码库健康评估。

Result: 实验揭示了两个重要发现：1）广泛使用的孤立PR评估存在严重的性能膨胀，偏差可达20个百分点，因为忽略了先前低效或buggy代码的"溢出效应"；2）即使智能体成功解决问题，其生成的代码在认知复杂度和引入的技术债务方面也高于人类开发者。

Conclusion: 编程智能体的评估需要从单一PR转向多维度、长期视角的评估体系。孤立PR评估会严重高估智能体能力，且成功解决问题的智能体仍可能损害代码库长期健康，这对实际部署构成风险。

Abstract: Existing datasets for coding agents evaluate performance on isolated, single pull request (PR) tasks in a stateless manner, failing to capture the reality of real-world software development where code changes accumulate, technical debt accrues, and test suites grow over time. To bridge this gap, we introduce an automated coding task generation framework, which helps generate our dataset SWE-STEPS, that evaluates coding agents on long-horizon tasks through two realistic settings mirroring actual developer workflows: Conversational coding with iterative requests, and single-shot Project Requirement document (PRD)-based coding. Unlike existing datasets that evaluate agents on disjointed Pull Requests (PRs), our framework assesses performance across chains of dependent PRs, enabling evaluation of sequential execution, regression verification, and long-term repository health. We discover that widely used isolated PR evaluations yield inflated success rates, w.r.t. our settings - overshooting performance by as much as 20 percentage points - because they ignore the ``spillover'' effects of previous inefficient or buggy code. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that even when agents successfully resolve issues, they degrade repository health by generating code with higher cognitive complexity and technical debt compared to human developers, underscoring the necessity for multidimensional evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [Combining Static Code Analysis and Large Language Models Improves Correctness and Performance of Algorithm Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03048)
*Denis Neumüller,Sebastian Boll,David Schüler,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了将大语言模型(LLM)与静态代码分析相结合自动识别源代码中算法的效果，发现混合方法可减少72.39-97.50%的LLM调用，同时F1分数提升高达12个百分点，且LLM在标识符混淆后仍能识别大多数算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者花费大量时间理解源代码，自动识别算法能力可支持程序理解、维护并提升软件质量，因此研究如何有效识别源代码中的算法具有重要实践价值。

Method: 采用多组实验评估LLM与静态分析结合的效果，使用不同过滤模式进行测试；比较混合方法与独立使用的性能差异；测试多种提示策略；通过系统性标识符混淆实验研究命名信息依赖程度。

Result: 混合方法显著减少LLM调用(72.39-97.50%)，大幅降低运行时间，F1分数提升至多12个百分点；情境学习配合两个示例可在性能与效率间取得良好平衡(F1: 75-77%)；LLM对标识符命名存在一定鲁棒性，混淆后仍能识别大部分算法。

Conclusion: LLM与静态分析相结合的混合方法能有效降低运行时间并提升识别性能，证实了该混合策略在自动算法识别任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Context: Since it is well-established that developers spend a substantial portion of their time understanding source code, the ability to automatically identify algorithms within source code presents a valuable opportunity. This capability can support program comprehension, facilitate maintenance, and enhance overall software quality. Objective: We empirically evaluate how combining LLMs with static code analysis can improve the automated recognition of algorithms, while also evaluating their standalone performance and dependence on identifier names. Method: We perform multiple experiments evaluating the combination of LLMs with static analysis using different filter patterns. We compare this combined approach against their standalone performance under various prompting strategies and investigate the impact of systematic identifier obfuscation on classification performance and runtime. Results: The combination of LLMs with lightweight static analysis performs surprisingly well, reducing required LLM calls by 72.39-97.50% depending on the filter pattern. This not only lowers runtime significantly but also improves F1-scores by up to 12 percentage points (pp) compared to the baseline. Regarding the different prompting strategies, in-context learning with two examples provides an effective trade-off between classification performance and runtime efficiency, achieving F1-scores of 75-77% with only a modest increase in inference time. Lastly, we find that LLMs are not solely dependent on name-information as they are still able to identify most algorithm implementations when identifiers are obfuscated. Conclusion: By combining LLMs with static analysis, we achieve substantial reductions in runtime while simultaneously improving F1-scores, underscoring the value of a hybrid approach.

</details>


### [167] [SkillRT: Compiling Skills for Efficient Execution Everywhere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03088)
*Le Chen,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillRT系统，将技能视为代码、将LLM视为异构处理器，通过编译时能力分析与运行时优化，实现跨平台技能的可移植高效执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM Agent系统中，技能作为原始上下文被共享，导致同一技能在不同Agent平台上表现不一致。这种脆弱性严重损害了技能的可移植性和执行效率，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 1. 分析118,000个技能样本；2. 将技能需求分解为原语能力集；3. 测量各模型-框架组合的能力支持程度；4. 设计SkillRT系统：编译时进行基于能力的编译、环境绑定和并发提取；运行时执行JIT代码固化与自适应重编译优化。

Result: 在8种不同规模LLM和3种Agent框架上验证，覆盖SkillsBench及代表性技能任务。结果显示：任务完成率显著提升，Token消耗降低达40%，并行加速最高3.2倍，代码固化实现19-50倍延迟降低。

Conclusion: SkillRT通过编译优化和运行时自适应机制，有效解决了跨平台技能执行的可移植性和效率问题，为LLM Agent技能共享提供了系统级解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adopt skills as a reusable unit of composition. While skills are shared across diverse agent platforms, current systems treat them as raw context, causing the same skill to behave inconsistently for different agents. This fragility undermines skill portability and execution efficiency.
  To address this challenge, we analyze 118,000 skills and draw inspiration from traditional compiler design. We treat skills as code and LLMs as heterogeneous processors. To make portability actionable, we decompose a skill's requirements into a set of primitive capabilities, and measure how well each model-harness pair supports them. Based on these capability profiles, we propose SkillRT, a compilation and runtime system designed for portable and efficient skill execution. At compile time, SkillRT performs capability-based compilation, environment binding, and concurrency extraction. At runtime, SkillRT applies JIT code solidification and adaptive recompilation for performance optimization.
  We evaluate SkillRT across eight LLMs of varying scales and three agent harnesses, covering SkillsBench and representative skill tasks. Results demonstrate that SkillRT significantly improves task completion rates across different models and environments while reducing token consumption by up to 40%. In terms of performance, SkillRT achieves up to 3.2x speedup with enhanced parallelism, and 19-50x latency reduction through code solidification.

</details>


### [168] [PAFT: Preservation Aware Fine-Tuning for Minimal-Edit Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03113)
*Boyang Yang,Zijian Cai,Shunfu Jin,Haoye Tain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAFT通过保留感知微调减少LLM在程序修复中的过度编辑问题，通过对齐buggy和修复代码获取token级保留信号，结合全序列masking和编辑难度课程学习，在Defects4J和HumanEval-Java上提升pass@1最多65.6%，同时减少平均编辑距离32.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM进行程序修复时，经常生成超过必要范围的补丁，导致代码重写过多，增加代码审查和维护成本。这是因为大多数bug具有局部性，但标准监督微调缺乏明确的信号来指示哪些token应该保留、哪些应该修改。

Method: PAFT的核心方法包括三个关键组件：1) 通过对齐buggy代码和修复代码推导token级别的保留信号；2) 结合全序列masking策略；3) 应用编辑难度课程（edit-difficulty curriculum）进行训练。

Result: 在Defects4J和HumanEval-Java上，PAFT相比标准监督微调(StdFT)提升pass@1最多65.6%，同时减少平均编辑距离(AED)最多32.6%。在Defects4J+DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B上，PAFT也优于偏好学习方法AdaPatcher，将pass@1从5.9%提升至10.1%，中位数AED从61.0降至42.0。

Conclusion: PAFT能够保留稳定上下文并将编辑集中于错误区域，生成更小、更局部化、更合理的补丁。整个过程无需推理时搜索、重排或后处理，方法简洁有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective for automated program repair, but plausible patches that pass the full test suite often rewrite more code than necessary, increasing review and maintenance costs. This over-editing is common because most bugs are localized, while standard supervised fine-tuning provides no explicit signal about which tokens should be preserved and which should be changed. We propose PAFT, a preservation-aware fine-tuning method for minimal-edit program repair. PAFT derives token-level preservation signals by aligning buggy and fixed code, combines them with full-sequence masking, and applies an edit-difficulty curriculum. Across Defects4J and HumanEval-Java, PAFT improves pass@1 by up to 65.6% over standard supervised fine-tuning (StdFT) while reducing average edit distance (AED) by up to 32.6%. On Defects4J with DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B, PAFT also outperforms AdaPatcher, a strong preference-based repair baseline, improving pass@1 from 5.9% to 10.1% while reducing median AED from 61.0 to 42.0. Overall, PAFT preserves stable context and concentrates edits on faulty regions, yielding smaller, more localized, plausible patches without inference-time search, reranking, or post-processing.

</details>


### [169] [AI-Assisted Unit Test Writing and Test-Driven Code Refactoring: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03135)
*Ema Smolic,Mario Brcic,Luka Hobor,Mihael Kovac*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究利用编码模型（AI）自动生成单元测试并辅助安全重构原型/MVP代码库的案例。通过迭代生成测试捕获现有系统行为，再在开发者监督下进行模型辅助重构，在数小时内生成了近16,000行可靠单元测试，关键模块达到78%的分支覆盖率，显著降低了大规模重构的回归风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多软件系统以原型或MVP形式开发，重速度而轻可维护性，导致代码难以修改。在AI辅助编程时代，这带来了巨大机会成本。传统重构风险高、缺乏充分测试覆盖，需要新方法实现安全演进。

Method: 案例研究：使用编码模型自动生成单元测试，随后在开发者监督下进行模型辅助重构。迭代式生成测试以捕获现有系统行为，通过通过的测试验证重构变更的有效性。研究记录了约束重构变更的方式、两阶段的错误与局限、效率提升幅度、手动介入时机以及模型价值对齐弱点的应对策略。

Result: 生成近16,000行可靠单元测试（耗时数小时而非数周），关键模块达到78%的分支覆盖率，大规模重构时回归风险显著降低。

Conclusion: 软件工程正朝着经验科学方向转变，需强调数据收集和约束机制以支持快速、安全的迭代。AI辅助测试生成与重构验证为遗留代码的安全演进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Many software systems originate as prototypes or minimum viable products (MVPs), developed with an emphasis on delivery speed and responsiveness to changing requirements rather than long-term code maintainability. While effective for rapid delivery, this approach can result in codebases that are difficult to modify, presenting a significant opportunity cost in the era of AI-assisted or even AI-led programming. In this paper, we present a case study of using coding models for automated unit test generation and subsequent safe refactoring, with proposed code changes validated by passing tests. The study examines best practices for iteratively generating tests to capture existing system behavior, followed by model-assisted refactoring under developer supervision. We describe how this workflow constrained refactoring changes, the errors and limitations observed in both phases, the efficiency gains achieved, when manual intervention was necessary, and how we addressed the weak value misalignment we observed in models. Using this approach, we generated nearly 16,000 lines of reliable unit tests in hours rather than weeks, achieved up to 78\% branch coverage in critical modules, and significantly reduced regression risk during large-scale refactoring. These results illustrate software engineering's shift toward an empirical science, emphasizing data collection and constraining mechanisms that support fast, safe iteration.

</details>


### [170] [From Industry Claims to Empirical Reality: An Empirical Study of Code Review Agents in Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03196)
*Kowshik Chowdhury,Dipayan Banik,K M Ferdous,Shazibul Islam Shamim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析AIDev的19,450个PRs，发现仅使用CRA的PR合并率（45.20%）显著低于仅使用人工审查的PR（68.37%）。60.2%的CRA审查PR信号比处于0-30%低区间，12/13个CRA的平均信号比低于60%，表明自动化审查反馈存在大量噪声。CRA应作为人工审查的补充而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主编码智能体生成代码的规模激增，代码审查智能体（CRAs）已成为开发工作流中的常规把关角色。业界宣称CRAs可独立处理开源仓库中80%的PRs，但CRA反馈质量如何影响PR结果的经验性证据仍然有限。

Method: 从AIDev的19,450个PRs中，筛选出3,109个处于评论审查状态的不同PRs，对比仅人工审查与仅CRA审查的差异。进一步分析98个已关闭的仅CRA审查PRs，评估低信噪比是否导致PR被遗弃。采用信号-噪声比分析评估CRA生成评论的质量。

Result: 仅CRA审查的PRs合并率为45.20%，比仅人工审查的PRs（68.37%）低23.17个百分点，且遗弃率显著更高。信号分析显示，60.2%的已关闭仅CRA审查PRs落入0-30%信号区间，13个CRAs中有12个平均信号比低于60%，表明自动化审查反馈存在大量噪声。

Conclusion: 无人工监督的CRAs通常产生低信号反馈，这与更高的PR遗弃率相关。对于实践者，CRA应增强而非取代人工审查者，人工参与对于有效且可操作的代码审查仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are generating code at an unprecedented scale, with OpenAI Codex alone creating over 400,000 pull requests (PRs) in two months. As agentic PR volumes increase, code review agents (CRAs) have become routine gatekeepers in development workflows. Industry reports claim that CRAs can manage 80% of PRs in open source repositories without human involvement. As a result, understanding the effectiveness of CRA reviews is crucial for maintaining developmental workflows and preventing wasted effort on abandoned pull requests. However, empirical evidence on how CRA feedback quality affects PR outcomes remains limited. The goal of this paper is to help researchers and practitioners understand when and how CRAs influence PR merge success by empirically analyzing reviewer composition and the signal quality of CRA-generated comments. From AIDev's 19,450 PRs, we analyze 3,109 unique PRs in the commented review state, comparing human-only versus CRA-only reviews. We examine 98 closed CRA-only PRs to assess whether low signal-to-noise ratios contribute to abandonment. CRA-only PRs achieve a 45.20% merge rate, 23.17 percentage points lower than human-only PRs (68.37%), with significantly higher abandonment. Our signal-to-noise analysis reveals that 60.2% of closed CRA-only PRs fall into the 0-30% signal range, and 12 of 13 CRAs exhibit average signal ratios below 60%, indicating substantial noise in automated review feedback. These findings suggest that CRAs without human oversight often generate low-signal feedback associated with higher abandonment. For practitioners, our results indicate that CRAs should augment rather than replace human reviewers and that human involvement remains critical for effective and actionable code review.

</details>
