### [171] [Robust Learning with Optimal Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02555)
*Guy Blanc*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We construct algorithms with optimal error for learning with adversarial noise. The overarching theme of this work is that the use of \textsl{randomized} hypotheses can substantially improve upon the best error rates achievable with deterministic hypotheses.
  - For $η$-rate malicious noise, we show the optimal error is $\frac{1}{2} \cdot η/(1-η)$, improving on the optimal error of deterministic hypotheses by a factor of $1/2$. This answers an open question of Cesa-Bianchi et al. (JACM 1999) who showed randomness can improve error by a factor of $6/7$.
  - For $η$-rate nasty noise, we show the optimal error is $\frac{3}{2} \cdot η$ for distribution-independent learners and $η$ for fixed-distribution learners, both improving upon the optimal $2 η$ error of deterministic hypotheses. This closes a gap first noted by Bshouty et al. (Theoretical Computer Science 2002) when they introduced nasty noise and reiterated in the recent works of Klivans et al. (NeurIPS 2025) and Blanc et al. (SODA 2026).
  - For $η$-rate agnostic noise and the closely related nasty classification noise model, we show the optimal error is $η$, improving upon the optimal $2η$ error of deterministic hypotheses.
  All of our learners have sample complexity linear in the VC-dimension of the concept class and polynomial in the inverse excess error. All except for the fixed-distribution nasty noise learner are time efficient given access to an oracle for empirical risk minimization.

</details>


### [172] [Non-Signaling Locality Lower Bounds for Dominating Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02582)
*Noah Fleming,Max Hopkins,Yuichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在非信号模型中证明了最小支配集问题O(logΔ)近似算法需要Ω(log n/(logΔ·poly log log Δ))的局部性下界，并进一步给出O(log^β Δ)近似需要Ω(log n/logΔ)下界，由此导出量子LOCAL模型中与度无关的Ω(√(log n/log log n))下界。证明依赖于两个新的标签覆盖低灵敏度下界，通过Impagliazzo-Kabanets-Wigoderson风格并行重复与度约化以及Dinur-Harsha框架的灵敏度保持变体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小支配集的局部性下界存在显著缺口：Chang-Li证明常数因子近似需要Ω(log n)局部性，但Kuhn-Moscibroda-Wattenhofer的算法突破了此界限，仅得到关于度的更弱下界min{logΔ/log logΔ, √(log n/log log n)}。核心问题在于：O(logΔ)近似是否必然需要Ω(log n)局部性，以及这些界限能否扩展到LOCAL之外的模型？

Method: 通过两步新标签覆盖灵敏度下界证明：(1) Impagliazzo-Kabanets-Wigderson风格的并行重复与度约化技术；(2) Dinur-Harsha框架的灵敏度保持重构。核心工具为Fleming-Yoshida的灵敏度-局部性转化定理，将标签覆盖的灵敏度下界转化为支配集问题在非信号模型中的局部性下界。

Result: 在非信号模型中：(1) 任何O(logΔ)近似的非信号分布需要局部性Ω(log n/(logΔ·poly log log Δ))；(2) 对某些β∈(0,1)，任何O(log^βΔ)近似的非信号分布需要局部性Ω(log n/logΔ)。推论：在量子LOCAL模型中，对O(log^βΔ)近似算法得到与度无关的下界Ω(√(log n/log log n))。

Conclusion: 本研究在非信号模型中取得突破，首次证明了O(logΔ)近似需要显著的局部性下界，并将其推广到量子LOCAL模型获得与度无关的下界。技术贡献包括两个新的标签覆盖低灵敏度下界，为分布式近似算法的复杂度理解提供了新工具。

Abstract: Minimum dominating set is a basic local covering problem and a core task in distributed computing. Despite extensive study, in the classic LOCAL model there exist significant gaps between known algorithms and lower bounds. Chang and Li prove an $Ω(\log n)$-locality lower bound for a constant factor approximation, while Kuhn--Moscibroda--Wattenhofer gave an algorithm beating this bound beyond $\log Δ$-approximation, along with a weaker lower bound for this degree-dependent setting scaling roughly with $\min\{\log Δ/\log\log Δ,\sqrt{\log n/\log\log n}\}$. Unfortunately, this latter bound is weak for small $Δ$, and never recovers the Chang--Li bound, leaving central questions: does $O(\log Δ)$-approximation require $Ω(\log n)$ locality, and do such bounds extend beyond LOCAL?
  In this work, we take a major step toward answering these questions in the non-signaling model, which strictly subsumes the LOCAL, quantum-LOCAL, and bounded-dependence settings. We prove every $O(\logΔ)$-approximate non-signaling distribution for dominating set requires locality $Ω(\log n/(\logΔ\cdot \mathrm{poly}\log\logΔ))$. Further, we show for some $β\in (0,1)$, every $O(\log^βΔ)$-approximate non-signaling distribution requires locality $Ω(\log n/\logΔ)$, which combined with the KMW bound yields a degree-independent $Ω(\sqrt{\log n/\log\log n})$ quantum-LOCAL lower bound for $O(\log^βΔ)$-approximation algorithms.
  The proof is based on two new low-soundness sensitivity lower bounds for label cover, one via Impagliazzo--Kabanets--Wigderson-style parallel repetition with degree reduction and one from a sensitivity-preserving reworking of the Dinur--Harsha framework, together with the reductions from label cover to set cover to dominating set and the sensitivity-to-locality transfer theorem of Fleming and Yoshida.

</details>


### [173] [Stochastic Function Certification with Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02611)
*Rohan Ghuge,Jai Moondra,Mohit Singh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究相关分布下的随机布尔函数认证(SBFC)问题，针对在未知激活场景下通过探针认证布尔函数值的问题给出了近似算法。核心贡献包括：一般分布下O(log n)近似(下界为NP难)、均匀拟阵常数因子4.642近似、图探测问题O(log k)自适应近似算法，显著改进此前多项式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有工作假设变量独立，而实际应用中变量常存在相关性。研究相关分布下的SBFC问题更具实际意义。目标是找到最小化期望探针数的算法来认证f(S)=1。

Method: 针对不同拟阵结构设计近似算法：(1)对任意分布使用非自适应O(log n)近似；(2)对均匀拟阵利用负相关性假设改进至常数因子；(3)对图探测问题设计自适应O(log k)近似算法。

Result: 给出三组结果：①任意分布下O(log n)近似且在分区拟阵下NP难以下界成立；②均匀拟阵4.642近似，负相关时改进至2近似；③图探测k-均匀拟阵自适应O(log k)近似。

Conclusion: 证明了O(log n)近似在任意分布下-tight（除非P=NP），并将k-均匀拟阵的自适应下界从Ω(poly(n))显著改进至O(log k)。

Abstract: We study the Stochastic Boolean Function Certification (SBFC) problem, where we are given $n$ Bernoulli random variables $\{X_e: e \in U\}$ on a ground set $U$ of $n$ elements with joint distribution $p$, a Boolean function $f: 2^U \to \{0, 1\}$, and an (unknown) scenario $S = \{e \in U: X_e = 1\}$ of active elements sampled from $p$. We seek to probe the elements one-at-a-time to reveal if they are active until we can certify $f(S) = 1$, while minimizing the expected number of probes. Unlike most previous results that assume independence, we study correlated distributions $p$ and give approximation algorithms for several classes of functions $f$.
  When $f(S)$ is the indicator function for whether $S$ is the spanning set of a given matroid, our problem reduces to finding a basis of active elements of a matroid by probing elements. We give a non-adaptive $O(\log n)$-approximation algorithm for arbitrary distributions $p$, and show that this is tight up to constants unless P $=$ NP, even for partition matroids. For uniform matroids, we give constant factor $4.642$-approximation ([BBFT20]) that can be further improved to a $2$-approximation if additionally the random variables are negatively correlated for the case of $1$-uniform matroid.
  We also give an adaptive $O(\log k)$-approximation algorithm for SBFC for $k$-uniform matroids for the Graph Probing problem, where we seek to probe the edges of a graph one-at-a-time until we find $k$ active edges. The underlying distribution on edges arises from (hidden) independent vertex random variables, with an edge being active if at least one of its endpoints is active. This significantly improves over the information-theoretic lower bound on $Ω(\mathrm{poly}(n))$ ([JGM19]) for adaptive algorithms for $k$-uniform matroids with arbitrary distributions.

</details>


### [174] [Engineering Algorithms for Dynamic Greedy Set Cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03152)
*Amitai Uzrad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次对动态集合覆盖问题的最新理论算法进行了全面的实验评估，填补了理论设计与实际性能之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态集合覆盖问题在近十年取得了重要的理论突破，但缺乏实验验证这些算法在实际场景中的表现，导致理论设计与实际应用之间存在显著差距。

Method: 实现并评估了四种基于贪心策略的动态算法，这些算法源自最新的理论框架（GKKP、SU、SUZ），并通过简化和优化其中复杂的子程序以提升实用性。评估维度包括解的质量、更新时间和 recourse（每次更新的解变化次数）。

Result: 提供了首个关于哪些算法策略在现实场景中最有效的实证见解，并揭示了参数β（质量与效率之间的权衡）对各算法的影响规律。

Conclusion: 该研究为动态集合覆盖算法的实际应用提供了重要指导，表明通过适当简化复杂子程序可使理论算法更具实用性。

Abstract: In the dynamic set cover problem, the input is a dynamic universe of elements and a fixed collection of sets. As elements are inserted or deleted, the goal is to efficiently maintain an approximate minimum set cover. While the past decade has seen significant theoretical breakthroughs for this problem, a notable gap remains between theoretical design and practical performance, as no comprehensive experimental study currently exists to validate these results.
  In this paper, we bridge this gap by implementing and evaluating four greedy-based dynamic algorithms across a diverse range of real-world instances. We derive our implementations from state-of-the-art frameworks (such as GKKP, STOC 2017; SU, STOC 2023; SUZ, FOCS 2024), which we simplify by identifying and modifying intricate subroutines that optimize asymptotic bounds but hinder practical performance. We evaluate these algorithms based on solution quality (set cover size) and efficiency, which comprises update time (the time required to update the solution following each insertion or deletion) and recourse (the number of changes made to the solution per update). Each algorithm uses a parameter $β$ to balance quality against efficiency; we investigate the influence of this tradeoff parameter on each algorithm and then perform a comparative analysis to evaluate the algorithms against each other. Our results provide the first practical insights into which algorithmic strategies provide the most value in realistic scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [High Volatility and Action Bias Distinguish LLMs from Humans in Group Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02578)
*Sahaj Singh Maini,Robert L. Goldstone,Zoran Tiganj*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究比较了大语言模型(LLM)与人类在群体协调任务中的表现，发现在"群体二进制搜索"游戏中，LLM无法像人类一样随时间适应和稳定行为，表现出过度切换行为；且更丰富的反馈信息对人类帮助更大，但对LLM影响较小。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM是否能像人类一样展示适应性协调能力，以及它们是否采用与人类相同的策略。这是一个关于LLM群体协作能力的基础性问题。



Method: 设计了一个n人共同利益博弈游戏——"群体二进制搜索"。玩家需独立提交数值，目标是使所有人的数值之和等于随机分配的目标数字。在无直接通信的情况下，玩家依靠群体反馈迭代调整提交值直到达到目标。



Result: 与人类不同，LLM无法在游戏中改进表现，表现出过度切换行为，损害群体收敛。更丰富的反馈（如数值误差大小）对人类有显著帮助，但对LLM影响很小。



Conclusion: 通过以人类表现为基准并使用机制层面的指标（反应性扩展、切换动态、游戏间学习），研究揭示了人类与LLM群体的差异，并为缩小协调差距提供了行为诊断工具。



Abstract: Humans exhibit remarkable abilities to coordinate in groups. As large language models (LLMs) become more capable, it remains an open question whether they can demonstrate comparable adaptive coordination and whether they use the same strategies as humans. To investigate this, we compare LLM and human performance on a common-interest game with imperfect monitoring: Group Binary Search. In this n-player game, participants need to coordinate their actions to achieve a common objective. Players independently submit numerical values in an effort to collectively sum to a randomly assigned target number. Without direct communication, they rely on group feedback to iteratively adjust their submissions until they reach the target number. Our findings show that, unlike humans who adapt and stabilize their behavior over time, LLMs often fail to improve across games and exhibit excessive switching, which impairs group convergence. Moreover, richer feedback (e.g., numerical error magnitude) benefits humans substantially but has small effects on LLMs. Taken together, by grounding the analysis in human baselines and mechanism-level metrics, including reactivity scaling, switching dynamics, and learning across games, we point to differences in human and LLM groups and provide a behaviorally grounded diagnostic for closing the coordination gap.

</details>


### [176] [Do Agent Societies Develop Intellectual Elites? The Hidden Power Laws of Collective Cognition in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02674)
*Kavana Venkatesh,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 首个大规模实证研究表明，LLM多智能体系统中的协调遵循重尾级联、通过优先附着集中于"知识精英"，且随系统规模增长产生更频繁的极端事件。这些效应由单一结构机制——整合瓶颈——耦合产生。提出DTI方法选择性增强整合，在协调失败处提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统规模化部署时收益递减或不稳定，但原因尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对协调动态的系统性大规模实证分析。

Method: 1) 提出原子事件级 formulations，将推理重构为协调级联；2) 分析超过150万次交互，涵盖不同任务、拓扑和规模；3) 识别三个耦合定律；4) 提出整合瓶颈机制解释现象；5) 设计DTI方法进行验证。

Result: 发现三个耦合定律：1) 协调遵循重尾级联分布；2) 通过优先附着机制协调集中于"知识精英"；3) 系统规模增长导致极端事件频率增加。这些效应由整合瓶颈机制统一解释：协调扩展随系统规模增长，但整合不增长，形成大但弱整合的推理过程。

Conclusion: 建立了集体认知的定量规律，识别协调结构为理解和改进规模化多智能体智能的基础性、未被测量的新维度。DTI方法有效改善协调失败处的性能，且不抑制大规模推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed as interacting agent societies, yet scaling these systems often yields diminishing or unstable returns, the causes of which remain poorly understood. We present the first large-scale empirical study of coordination dynamics in LLM-based multi-agent systems, introducing an atomic event-level formulation that reconstructs reasoning as cascades of coordination. Analyzing over 1.5 Million interactions across tasks, topologies, and scales, we uncover three coupled laws: coordination follows heavy-tailed cascades, concentrates via preferential attachment into intellectual elites, and produces increasingly frequent extreme events as system size grows. We show that these effects are coupled through a single structural mechanism: an integration bottleneck, in which coordination expansion scales with system size while consolidation does not, producing large but weakly integrated reasoning processes. To test this mechanism, we introduce Deficit-Triggered Integration (DTI), which selectively increases integration under imbalance. DTI improves performance precisely where coordination fails, without suppressing large-scale reasoning. Together, our results establish quantitative laws of collective cognition and identify coordination structure as a fundamental, previously unmeasured axis for understanding and improving scalable multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [177] [Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning-based Joint Design of Low-Carbon P2P Market and Bidding Strategy in Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02728)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Sijie Wang,Lan Zhao,Qiyu Kang,Aniq Ashan,Yajuan Sun,Gaoxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种日内点对点(P2P)交易框架，通过多智能体强化学习(MARL)和分布式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(DEC-POMDP)，在保护微电网自主决策权的同时，利用市场清算机制引导其优先消纳本地可再生能源，实现个体经济利益与社区低碳目标的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源发电的不确定性和实时市场价格的不稳定性限制了清洁能源的有效利用；现有P2P和微电网协调方法通常依赖中心化优化或受限的协调规则，难以在实际应用中落地实施。

Method: 将微电网的决策过程建模为DEC-POMDP问题，并采用MARL框架求解；引入新型市场清算机制提供宏观调节，激励微电网优先消纳本地可再生能源从而减少碳排放。

Result: 仿真结果表明，自利竞价策略与P2P市场设计的结合显著提升了可再生能源利用率，降低了对高碳排放外部电力的依赖。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了本地自治、自我利益追求与社区级经济和环境效益改善的平衡整合。

Abstract: The challenges of the uncertainties in renewable energy generation and the instability of the real-time market limit the effective utilization of clean energy in microgrid communities. Existing peer-to-peer (P2P) and microgrid coordination approaches typically rely on certain centralized optimization or restrictive coordination rules which are difficult to be implemented in real-life applications. To address the challenge, we propose an intraday P2P trading framework that allows self-interested microgrids to pursue their economic benefits, while allowing the market operator to maximize the social welfare, namely the low carbon emission objective, of the entire community. Specifically, the decision-making processes of the microgrids are formulated as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (DEC-POMDP) and solved using a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework. Such an approach grants each microgrid a high degree of decision-making autonomy, while a novel market clearing mechanism is introduced to provide macro-regulation, incentivizing microgrids to prioritize local renewable energy consumption and hence reduce carbon emissions. Simulation results demonstrate that the combination of the self-interested bidding strategy and the P2P market design helps significantly improve renewable energy utilization and reduce reliance on external electricity with high carbon-emissions. The framework achieves a balanced integration of local autonomy, self-interest pursuit, and improved community-level economic and environmental benefits.

</details>
